#### Profile: Heurist X402 Vending Machine (vending machine only, not the company)
**One-line:** A launchpad whose x402 payments buy locked tokens in a 14-day sale, then graduate into a pair with HEU. On-chain data shows 20 launches in a three-week window in Oct-Nov 2025 and none since. [docs](https://docs.heurist.ai/x402-products/vending-machine)
**x402 role:** Buyers pay USDC by x402 to the vault address. Operators then record the purchase on-chain. The x402scan series pulled for this note shows 105,587 payments worth $43,310.16 from 571 buyers to that vault over 34 active days, 2025-10-28 to 2026-01-16. [vault](https://basescan.org/address/0xca85edab3ede15059fdabf27d861a942196ccccf)
**Chains:** Base live; BNB Chain and X Layer listed as coming; the front end is also listed as coming. [repo](https://github.com/heurist-network/x402-vending-machine)
**Token:**
- Each launch deploys a fixed 1B-supply ERC-20 (80% public sale, 10% pool, 8% creator, 2% platform). HEU (0xEF22cb48B8483dF6152e1423b19dF5553BbD818b) is the swap and pair asset. [docs](https://docs.heurist.ai/x402-products/vending-machine)
- Contract facts (Base RPC and Sourcify, 2026-10-11): the manager contract 0x76e16f457083a80b3c24fd2b1ba8ccad60841c04 was created 2025-10-27 18:45:39 UTC (same block as the vault) with source verified [Sourcify](https://sourcify.dev/server/v2/contract/8453/0x76e16f457083a80b3c24fd2b1ba8ccad60841c04?fields=abi). It has recorded 20 launches (ids 1 to 20), created 2025-10-28 to 2025-11-14; the head block checked was 2026-10-11 12:47 UTC and none were added since. I confirmed the count independently: `nextLaunchId()` returns 20 and the source increments it before each launch (`id = ++nextLaunchId`). [BaseScan](https://basescan.org/address/0x76e16f457083a80b3c24fd2b1ba8ccad60841c04)

**Launches actually made:**
- Sizes: 8 test (4 USDC target), 10 small (4,000 USDC), 2 large (40,000 USDC). Sales run on fixed prices.
- Graduated (5): New Test Token (test), DOG, Vending Machine (VENDING), Government Shutdown (SHUTDOWN), x402mascot. DOG was created 2025-10-28 16:02 UTC and its pair at 17:52 UTC. Pool liquidity today is a few hundred dollars: about $400 for DOG and about $182 for SHUTDOWN (GeckoTerminal); the other three were not read. [GeckoTerminal DOG](https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/networks/base/tokens/0xaa9abdcdb1789bd7df555ed5dc33e4bb845505fe/pools)
- Unfinished: 8 launches (HAMSTER, BONG, Cross Chain Bridge, DEBUG, Basex402, Number Go Up, Not A Money Printer, Test Token 2) hold 429 USDC of recorded buyer contributions. The refund path is operator-only. The vault holds only 6.0 USDC (I re-read the balance on 2026-10-11 and got 6.0). Whether the gap was withdrawn through an admin function is not confirmed because log queries were blocked. [BaseScan](https://basescan.org/address/0xca85edab3ede15059fdabf27d861a942196ccccf)
- The wallet that deployed the manager also created launches 1 and 2 (tests). [BaseScan](https://basescan.org/address/0x7b5bac4d9c1a45460db80bc794a567c19106b8d3)

**Team:** Heurist is profiled elsewhere. GitHub commits are by account wjw12 (3 commits). On-chain, the default admin is an EOA (0xef06e4dd..., nonce 461) and three operator addresses control launches. Not tied to any person. [repo](https://github.com/heurist-network/x402-vending-machine), [BaseScan](https://basescan.org/address/0xef06e4dd7408c911489e8b258839230ff8798d16)
**Track record and pivots:** Repo created 2025-10-24, two days after the PING mint. Contracts deployed 2025-10-27. Last commit 2025-11-02. First launch 2025-10-28, last 2025-11-14. [repo](https://github.com/heurist-network/x402-vending-machine)
**Trend-hopping score (1-5):** 3. A quick reaction to the x402 token narrative by an established team, with real contracts but a short run. Company-level scoring belongs to the Heurist profile. [repo](https://github.com/heurist-network/x402-vending-machine)
**Funding:** Out of scope.
**Product and traction:** 20 launches, 5 graduated, 3 weeks of use. Cross-check, sibling table row 311: vending-machine origin 105.6K tx / $43.3K, which equals the vault's all-time total in this note's x402scan pull. Flag: recorded purchases total only about $16.4K (four small graduated sales at $4,000, one test at $4, plus 429 unresolved), so most of the $43.3K was not recorded as sale purchases; nine single-buyer days carry $25.7K of it. vending-machine.heurist.xyz returned HTTP 522 on 2026-10-11. [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md), [site](https://vending-machine.heurist.xyz)
**Last six months (2026-04-11 to 2026-10-11):** 0 commits, 0 launches and 0 x402 payments to the vault after 2026-01-16. [repo](https://github.com/heurist-network/x402-vending-machine)
**Direction:** The README says other chains and a front end are coming. No activity supports it. **Competitors:** Clanker, Virtuals, Pump.fun-style launchers, and the other tools in this section (opinion).
**Green and red flags:**
- Green: source verified; a fixed supply per launch; real graduations occurred.
- Red: the docs claim "Immutable Contracts - No upgrade keys or admin functions", "Zero Admin Keys - Treasury has no privileged access" and "rug pulls impossible" ([docs, read 2026-10-11](https://docs.heurist.ai/x402-products/vending-machine)). The verified source of the manager contract has an admin role that can withdraw all vault USDC (`emergencyWithdrawUSDC`) or refund any amount (`adminRefund`), plus operator-only functions for launches, purchases, graduation, refunds and the HEU price (I read the verified source on Sourcify, 2026-10-11). [Sourcify](https://sourcify.dev/server/v2/contract/8453/0x76e16f457083a80b3c24fd2b1ba8ccad60841c04?fields=sources)
- Red: the docs' address labels look wrong. The address labelled VendingMachine (0xca85...) is the TreasuryVault (`vault()` on the manager returns it), and the address labelled TreasuryVault (0x7d9d...) has no code and has sent 130 transactions (checked 2026-10-11). [BaseScan](https://basescan.org/address/0x7d9d1821d15b9e0b8ab98a058361233e255e405d)
- Red: vault shortfall against recorded contributions; dormant for 11 months.

**Verdict:** Likely abandoned; momentum Stalled. Opinion: the machine did launch and graduate a handful of tokens in its first three weeks. Nothing has happened since, the front end is down, and the docs overstate how trustless it is.
**Tracking card:** Docs docs.heurist.ai/x402-products/vending-machine · GitHub heurist-network/x402-vending-machine · manager 0x76e16f457083a80b3c24fd2b1ba8ccad60841c04 · vault 0xca85edab3ede15059fdabf27d861a942196ccccf · HEU 0xEF22cb48B8483dF6152e1423b19dF5553BbD818b
**Sources (accessed 2026-10-11):** https://docs.heurist.ai/x402-products/vending-machine · https://github.com/heurist-network/x402-vending-machine · https://basescan.org/address/0x76e16f457083a80b3c24fd2b1ba8ccad60841c04 · https://basescan.org/address/0xca85edab3ede15059fdabf27d861a942196ccccf · https://sourcify.dev/server/v2/contract/8453/0x76e16f457083a80b3c24fd2b1ba8ccad60841c04?fields=sources · https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/networks/base/tokens/0xaa9abdcdb1789bd7df555ed5dc33e4bb845505fe/pools · Base public RPC (mainnet.base.org) calls on 2026-10-11 · x402scan public API series

#### Other x402 launch tools (one-liners)
- **1ly.store:** x402 marketplace for agents on Base and Solana, with token launches on Bags.fm (Solana), so that part is off-Base. Its MCP server repo, created 2026-01-29, has 27 commits and 3 stars; the last commit is 2026-03-05. [repo](https://github.com/1lystore/1ly-mcp-server)
- **ConwayPad:** An AI chat that deploys Clanker tokens on Base. x402 pays only for its AI inference, not for launches. The repo (created 2026-02-22) has 5 commits and none since. Likely stale. [repo](https://github.com/jesenliam/conwaypad)
- **X402.Fun:** Agent-only meme launchpad on Solana, devnet, proprietary license. The repo (created 2026-03-11) has 157 commits, the last on 2026-03-19. Off-Base. [repo](https://github.com/LithiumEinsteinium/X402.Fun)
- **Hello 402:** Token launch platform on X Layer; the team is undisclosed and the listing says it has no native token. Off-Base. [Bitget listing](https://web3.bitget.com/es-AR/dapp/hello-402-31894)
- **XFOUR:** Fair-launch platform on BNB Smart Chain; mint with 1 USD4, refunds if criteria are missed; no team listed. Off-Base. [DappBay](https://dappbay.bnbchain.org/detail/xfour)
- **MNTx402:** No primary page found, and a GitHub repository search returned nothing. Solana per the brief. Off-Base, unconfirmed.

#### Observations across these profiles (Regents Labs, x420, x402.team, Covalent SpeedRun, Quick Intel, Heurist Vending Machine)
- **Reaction speed to the PING mint (2025-10-22/23):** x420 token 2025-10-23; Heurist machine repo 2025-10-24; x402 token and the separate x402 mint token 2025-10-25; Heurist contracts 2025-10-27; SpeedRun launch post 2025-10-30; REGENT 2025-11-06. The Regents founder had x402 repos from Aug to Oct 2025, before the wave.
- **Clanker is the usual token plumbing:** REGENT (SDK), x420 (via a Farcaster bot), x402.team (direct call), ConwayPad, SpeedRun and Tator. x402 is the payment gate in front. Heurist's machine is the exception, with its own ERC-20s.
- **Deployers:** no address is shared across these projects. The x420 sender looks like a shared relayer (nonce 422,604). The x402.team deployer has one Clanker token, per the sibling note.
- **People overlap:** only microchipgnu with the x420 repo. The sibling table lists x402MKT (LOOP) 0xa763b711...b07 with no x402 footprint; it is profiled in this section.
- **Narrative history in public repos:** the Regents founder (XMTP, frames, agent research, x402), the x420 owner (Stacks NFTs, Bitte agents, hackathon apps, OpenClaw forks), Quick Intel (scanner, agent studio, OpenClaw skills), Covalent (data API, AI agent SDK, SpeedRun, x402, Hyperliquid).
- **x402 use faded:** the x420 payee ended 2025-11-12, the Heurist vault 2026-01-16, GoldRush x402 is still on testnet, and Quick Intel is unmeasured.
- **Claims versus code:** Heurist docs say no admin keys while the verified contract has an admin (independently confirmed); Quick Intel's marketed launch fee and Flaunch support are not in official sources.
- **Could not confirm because WebSearch was unavailable:** all X posts and follower data (x.com is not fetchable); the founding history of Animata, Regents Club and regent.cx; round sizes for Covalent, Regents and Quick Intel; whether SpeedRun launched any Clanker token and why it is paused; the number of x402.team mints and any Heurist admin withdrawals (public RPCs blocked log queries); the Quick Intel team; site uptime for domains a local network filter later blocked for the sub-agent (autolaunch.sh, techtree.sh, patchbay.help, keyfleet.ai, regent.cx, 1ly.store, clanker.world; not bypassed).

#### Profile: APINow.fun ($APINOW)
**One-line:** Pay-per-call API marketplace for AI agents run by one named founder. It lists 5,377 endpoints, about 97% of them platform-generated, paid in USDC through x402. Its token layer has one live API token plus the platform token. [stats](https://www.apinow.fun/api/stats), [endpoints](https://www.apinow.fun/api/endpoints?limit=500)
**x402 role:** Each call is an x402 "exact" payment in USDC on Base, typically $0.01 to $0.03. The `/.well-known/x402` file lists 5,368 resources (5,363 Base, 3 Solana, 2 Ethereum) and names facilitator.payai.network. [x402 file](https://www.apinow.fun/.well-known/x402) The platform's own catalog names x402.dexter.cash as facilitator for its API routes. [catalog](https://www.apinow.fun/api/catalog) The npm SDK first depended on `@x402` libraries at v0.13.0 on 2026-02-19; before that (2025-01 to 2025-08) it used ethers-based custom payments. [npm](https://registry.npmjs.org/apinow-sdk) The 2025-02-05 one-pager describes transaction hashes as API keys and tokens burned per call; it does not mention x402 or Clanker. [one-pager](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1TX27bNh6njGRGa8vJcckntxvu0MF_TKTTSOufDjg0a8/mobilebasic)
**Chains:** Base (5,363 of 5,368 resources), plus a few legacy Solana and Ethereum endpoints. Tokenback also routes Linea mUSD. [x402 file](https://www.apinow.fun/.well-known/x402), [Tokenback](https://www.apinow.fun/tokenback)
**Token:**
- $APINOW; Base 0xE5dd257baB19CB8Cb6B3628C09B62465eF4b2B07. Created 2026-02-25 20:01:29 UTC (block 42,630,171) via the Clanker v4 factory, deployed on clanker.world. [creation tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0xfa850dd8f9ee0cef8b1d0c34b542bbdae4bc5dd02a47627719ab14565b5af2aa)
- Clanker's page shows 70% in the pool and 30% vaulted (unlock 2026-03-04, fully vested 2026-08-31), 100% of rewards to the creator wallet, a dynamic swap fee of 1% to 3% plus a 0.2% Clanker fee, and a sniper fee of 80% decaying to 5% over 120s. [Clanker page](https://www.clanker.world/clanker/0xE5dd257baB19CB8Cb6B3628C09B62465eF4b2B07)
- Utility: default target token for Tokenback. 72 holders, top 10 hold 99.86%, no trades in 24h (2026-10-11). [GeckoTerminal](https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/networks/base/tokens/0xE5dd257baB19CB8Cb6B3628C09B62465eF4b2B07/info)

**How tokenization works, and how many exist:** The roadmap says tokens launch for APIs with over 100 calls in a day and strong reliability. [roadmap](https://www.apinow.fun/roadmap) The one live example is TRANSLATE (0x5cd9...5b07, Clanker v3.1, 2025-05-29). A paired endpoint, `translate-TRANSLATE`, accepts the token as payment and has 864 lifetime calls. [creation tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0xd4f034995e397085907d7be23292d5a2feefdf19f5d8479c27862149e9c0af38), [endpoints](https://www.apinow.fun/api/endpoints?limit=500) The platform's `/api/tokens` list returns 0 tokens and `/coins` shows none. [tokens](https://www.apinow.fun/api/tokens) apimarketcap.com, linked from the site, does not resolve (NXDOMAIN). [DNS check](https://apimarketcap.com) So documented tokenized APIs: one, plus the platform token.
**Fees:** Platform API routes cost $0.01 per call; a "Get Featured" listing costs $25; an AI Factory build request costs $1; Tokenback shows a routing fee of about 0.5%. [catalog](https://www.apinow.fun/api/catalog), [Promotion](https://www.apinow.fun/promotion), [AI Factory](https://www.apinow.fun/ai-factory) The SDK says external x402 calls cost the upstream price plus a "small proxy fee" with no amount stated. [SDK README](https://github.com/1dolinski/apinow-sdk) No provider revenue share is published.
**Team:** Chris Dolinski, founder, doxxed. GitHub `1dolinski` shows that name and lists ApiNow.fun among his ventures. X @1dolinski is self-declared in the GitHub profile field and appears as the connected X account on his Farcaster profile. Farcaster @1dolinski (FID 10956) is linked by Clanker as the $APINOW creator. [GitHub](https://api.github.com/users/1dolinski), [Farcaster](https://api.warpcast.com/v2/user-by-username?username=1dolinski) Project X @apinowfun and Telegram t.me/apinowfun are linked from the site (Telegram showed 417 members). [site](https://www.apinow.fun), [Telegram](https://t.me/s/apinowfun) No other team members are named.
**Track record and pivots:** (all from GitHub repo creation dates and the sources named)
- 2021-04-15: `bitcloutoffers` repo. 2024-05-15: `genai` repo. 2024-10-18: `aidd` repo. 2024-10-27: `repochat.ai` repo.
- 2025-01-21: `apinow-sdk` repo. 2025-02-05: one-pager. 2025-05-29: TRANSLATE token.
- 2025-10-22: `x402-brandkit` repo. 2025-10-24: first x402scan payment.
- 2026-02-05: `gmclaw` repo and GMCLAW token (Bankr-deployed). 2026-02-13: VINI profile token. 2026-02-17: `fusionclaw` repo.
- 2026-02-19: SDK adopts x402. 2026-02-25: $APINOW.
- 2026-03: `hammock-ai`, `fastforms` and `odoai` repos. 2026-08-24: `balance` repo, built on MPP (Stripe/Tempo), not x402.
- Sources: [repos](https://api.github.com/users/1dolinski/repos?per_page=100&sort=created), [GMCLAW](https://www.clanker.world/clanker/0xe4Ecb4739b7B86c6892B7b3Fbf6CE23CA49c3B07), [balance](https://github.com/1dolinski/balance), [VINI tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0xdeb0f9b681ed31a668a4da87f110d293f72c365413a2793dac06c48c2f872759). The bio also lists Clutch.ca, Vibehut.io and GetFirstResponder.com; vibehut.io has no DNS address and the other two did not render. [bio](https://api.github.com/users/1dolinski)

**Trend-hopping score (1-5):** 3. The marketplace is 20 months old and has real code each time (61 npm versions, public API). The narrative has shifted: tokens burned per call, then Clanker API tokens, then x402-branded listing text, then evals, ERC-8004 sign-in, Tokenback and a bulk "AI Factory". The founder's side projects follow other trends (claw-named repos Feb 2026, local AI chat, Telegram bot, habit app, MPP). [AI Skills](https://www.apinow.fun/ai-skills)
**Funding:** None published on the site, docs or one-pager. The site claims "Consensus 2025 Pitch Winner" with no prize details; unverified. [site](https://www.apinow.fun)
**Product and traction:** `/api/stats` (2026-10-11): 16,646 calls, 5,377 endpoints; the sum of per-endpoint counts is 22,154, so the two disagree. [stats](https://www.apinow.fun/api/stats) The `ai-factory` namespace holds 4,170 endpoints and `gg402` 1,035; 5,310 endpoints share one payee wallet. 2,965 endpoints have exactly one call, 1,125 have none, and 21 have 100 or more (top: 1,221). Recent AI Factory batches average about one call per endpoint, which looks like automated test calls (inference). [endpoints](https://www.apinow.fun/api/endpoints?limit=500) x402scan for the payee wallet: 14,070 payments, $228.22 all-time, last payment 2026-10-09, at most 11 buyers a day. [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com/recipient/0x9257cD24721e3c06E9F7655b77C283bCD9652132) The sibling table gives 30-day 78 tx / $1 and all-time 14K / $228 (matched by the apinow.fun domain, so it covers the whole platform, not TRANSLATE payments), consistent with this pull (30-day 82 tx / $0.82). [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md) npm: 215 downloads in the last 30 days. [npm](https://api.npmjs.org/downloads/point/last-month/apinow-sdk)
**Last six months (2026-04-11 to 2026-10-11):** x402scan shows 8,538 payments / $176.10. Monthly: July $71.93, August $18.99, September $1.69, 2026-10-01 to 10-09 $0.06. [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com/recipient/0x9257cD24721e3c06E9F7655b77C283bCD9652132) SDK 0.26.0 to 0.30.1 shipped 2026-04-20 to 04-29. The public SDK repo had 2 commits (2026-04-20); `balance` had 3 (2026-08-24). Workflows (10 created 2026-04-20 to 05-25), Tokenback (beta) and Promotion appeared; AI Factory batches landed in 2026-05 and 2026-08; a third-party provider was added 2026-10-05. [commits](https://github.com/1dolinski/apinow-sdk/commits), [endpoints](https://www.apinow.fun/api/endpoints?limit=500)
**Direction:** The home page pitches discovery and evals for Claude Code and agents, with tokens for winning endpoints and Tokenback to create demand for $APINOW. The founder's newest repo uses MPP rather than x402. [site](https://www.apinow.fun)
**Competitors:** Coinbase CDP Bazaar (32,169 resources, 2,212 hosts), Orthogonal gateway (157 resources), Merit Systems' Stable family, Strale and Apify AGI. [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md)
**Green and red flags:** Green: named founder, continuous shipping since 2025-01, open API, verifiable data. Red: paid usage is tiny and falling; most endpoints are platform-generated; the token layer is nearly unused; the API Market Cap site is dead; the site's dated whitepaper is a 2025-02 one-pager; $APINOW is thinly held; the x402 libraries came late relative to the x402 launch (2025-05-06). The wallet Clanker shows as $APINOW's creator is admin of 66 Clanker tokens. On the recent four, fee shares go to other addresses (60/15/15/10), and on GLOBE the wallet takes 1%. This looks like a token-launch-for-others role (inference). [Clanker API](https://www.clanker.world/api/tokens/fetch-deployed-by-address?address=0xf447b4ccf3c4f8b158102cca9e88f48e467a21a6)
**Verdict:** Credible but small; momentum Slowing. Opinion: a real builder with a working API and negligible paid demand. Revenue and token mechanics are thinner than the pitch, and payments dropped sharply after July.
**Tracking card:** Website https://www.apinow.fun · docs https://www.apinow.fun/developers · GitHub https://github.com/1dolinski/apinow-sdk (npm `apinow-sdk`) · X @apinowfun (project), @1dolinski (founder) · Farcaster @1dolinski · Telegram t.me/apinowfun · tokens $APINOW 0xE5dd257baB19CB8Cb6B3628C09B62465eF4b2B07, TRANSLATE 0x5cd9166494673c15cbdfe8750def8bbab80a5b07
**Not confirmed:** Whether other API tokens exist on Clanker (the Clanker API stopped answering over TLS after the first calls); the private repo `1dolinski/apinow` named in npm metadata; posts on X, Telegram and Farcaster; the TRANSLATE Clanker page did not render.

#### Compact profiles: x402-mint family (PING-template tokens)
The six tokens below were deployed 2025-10-24 to 2025-10-27, in the week after PING. Four (BIP, MOOM, x420DOG, MAI) share PING's verified source template (ERC-20 plus EIP-3009 plus AccessControl; a `MINTER_ROLE`-only `batchMint(address[],bytes32[])` that credits a fixed amount per payment transaction hash up to a cap; an automatic Uniswap v4 pool at the cap; admin-only `withdrawToken`, `collectLpFees` and, in three of them, a one-time `emergencyWithdraw`); 叉肆零贰 uses a factory variant with the same `batchMint`; the x402 token at 0x4c15 is a plain ERC-20 with an owner-withdraw box. [Sourcify sources](https://sourcify.dev/server/v2/contract/8453/0x9b4dc4f56ec94e629252db7990f885a414a26083?fields=sources,abi) Payment totals were re-checked against this note's own x402scan pull (public API, 2026-10-11) and match.

Where the USDC went (Blockscout token-transfer API for each contract, read 2026-10-11; a withdrawal is not proof of wrongdoing, but I found no refund transfers). Across PING and these six contracts, about $603,468 of USDC left the contracts: $150,000 (25%) went into Uniswap v4 pools at the cap (PING 100,000 on 2025-10-22; x420DOG 50,000 on 2025-10-24) and about $453,468 (75%) was withdrawn by admin or owner accounts to their own addresses between 2025-10-25 and 2025-11-17: PING 77,574 (2025-11-17), BIP 122,287 (2025-10-26 to 10-29), MOOM 11,159 (2025-10-29), x420DOG 75,998 (2025-11-10), MAI 101,190 (2025-11-04), 叉肆零贰 55,225 (2025-10-27 and 11-04), x402 0x4c15 10,034 (2025-10-25/26). The x402scan totals for these seven payees add up to $553k, so the outflows exceed what x402scan counted by about $50k (x402scan may not index every facilitator).

##### Profile: BuildInPublic (BIP)
**One-line:** Anonymous one-day x402 mint token on Base. No site, team or product found. [GeckoTerminal](https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/networks/base/tokens/0x9b4dc4f56ec94e629252db7990f885a414a26083/info)
**x402 role:** The token contract was the payee. About $1 per payment earned 5,000 BIP, credited by a minter-role account through `batchMint`, with a cap of 100,000 mints (the PING template; see the family note above). x402scan shows 121,531 payments from 7,469 payers on 2025-10-26, $122,087. [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com/recipient/0x9b4dc4f56ec94e629252db7990f885a414a26083), [Sourcify source](https://sourcify.dev/server/v2/contract/8453/0x9b4dc4f56ec94e629252db7990f885a414a26083?fields=sources,abi)
**Chains:** Base. [creation tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0xd4deeb2c4633ba35c6bfe3402afc37f32d8b0e60864bac1e0a8295b0a4b7142e)
**Token:** BIP; Base 0x9b4dc4f56ec94e629252db7990f885a414a26083. Deployed 2025-10-25 23:38:37 UTC as a plain EOA deployment by 0x987410cc...e8ab. No utility stated and no real work. The verified source is a mint-and-seed contract copied from PING; its comments still say "PING". `mintCount()` reads 99,999 of the 100,000 cap, one short, so the automatic pool step never ran (`liquidityDeployed()` is false). Supply is 999,995,000, which is 99,999 mints x 5,000 plus 500,000,000: the extra 500M is what `emergencyWithdraw` mints to its caller. Blockscout shows `emergencyWithdraw` on 2025-10-26 10:39:21 UTC sending 115,562.001 USDC from the contract to the deployer wallet 0x987410CC...e8ab, then three more transfers to the same wallet (3,769 USDC at 11:00 and 1,183 USDC at 12:06 on 2025-10-26; 1,773 USDC on 2025-10-29), 122,287 USDC in all. [Blockscout USDC transfers](https://base.blockscout.com/api/v2/addresses/0x9b4dc4f56ec94e629252db7990f885a414a26083/token-transfers?type=ERC-20&filter=from) [creation tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0xd4deeb2c4633ba35c6bfe3402afc37f32d8b0e60864bac1e0a8295b0a4b7142e), [Blockscout](https://base.blockscout.com/address/0x9b4dc4f56ec94e629252db7990f885a414a26083?tab=contract)

#### Observations across the mint family, APINow.fun and the Bankr/B20 tokens
- **Template wave:** BIP, MOOM, x420DOG and MAI share PING-derived contract code, and 叉肆零贰 uses a launchpad variant. All six mint tokens (including x402 at 0x4c15) were deployed 2025-10-24 to 10-27.
- **Launchpad factory:** 0x64ad70ae... created 叉肆零贰 and one more contract on 2025-10-27; its mint endpoint is 402rush.fun. [Blockscout factory](https://base.blockscout.com/address/0x64ad70aeca1cd421896e9b4e9003e2475b165ecd)
- **Shared wallet:** 0xd9acd656... sent the creation transactions for TRANSLATE (2025-05-29), x420 (2025-10-23) and GMCLAW (2026-02-05). Clanker lists 307 tokens with that wallet as admin and GMCLAW's page says "Deployed using Bankr", so this looks like a shared Bankr launch wallet, not a shared team (inference). [Clanker API](https://www.clanker.world/api/tokens/fetch-deployed-by-address?address=0xd9acd656a5f1b519c9e76a2a6092265a74186e58)
- **B20 wave:** B20LaunchpadFactory verified 2026-07-11; Clanker "B20" test tokens 2026-07-08; HTTPBASE20 2026-07-16; HTTP402 2026-07-26. Within 5 to 15 days of the factory's verification, two new B20 tokens carried x402/HTTP names. [Blockscout](https://base.blockscout.com/address/0xa52ad458ce0282a971ecc71c051a32f28946bb9f?tab=contract)
- **Bankr as channel:** HermesOS, LUCA, X402ID and GMCLAW are Bankr launches; HTTPBASE20 is tagged Bankr Launchpad on GeckoTerminal; WalletChan wraps BankrWallet and integrates Bankr.
- **Domain churn:** x402mkt.com now serves Reloop; 402.dog serves an unrelated page; apimarketcap.com does not resolve; vibehut.io has no DNS address.
- **Same-name reuse:** six tokens named x402MKT on 2025-11-07; a second X402ID token; x420 versus x420DOG.
- **Founder wallet:** Clanker lists 66 tokens with the wallet it shows as $APINOW's creator as admin. [Clanker API](https://www.clanker.world/api/tokens/fetch-deployed-by-address?address=0xf447b4ccf3c4f8b158102cca9e88f48e467a21a6)
- **Corrections to the brief (mint family, APINow.fun and the Bankr/B20 tokens):** (1) The HTTP402 launcher is 0xa52ad458ce0282a971ecc71c051a32f28946bb9f (Blockscout name B20LaunchpadFactory), not 0x826a2b79...; only HTTPBASE20 went through 0x826a2b79..., which Blockscout names Clanker (a fork). (2) 402.dog and x402mkt.com no longer serve the project: 402.dog serves an unrelated page and x402mkt.com serves Reloop. (3) The sibling note's "x420" row is a different token (0x68e284f2...) from x420DOG (0xd0cb638d...).
- **Could not confirm (WebSearch unavailable and access limits):** any funding, press coverage or awards, including APINow's Consensus claim; X profiles (x.com returned HTTP 402 to the sub-agent, so no X handle was read directly; X handles above come from official site links, GitHub or listing profiles); sites that failed TLS from the sub-agent's machine (hivra.cloud, httpbase20.netlify.app, 402rush.fun, viniapp.xyz, and clanker.world after the first calls; none bypassed), so the Clanker API gave only one page of 5 tokens for each of two wallets; whether B20LaunchpadFactory belongs to o1, and who is behind the "cc0strategy" fork launcher; the Reloop attack claim, the $RELOOP contract address and the x402 Loops "14k+ mints" claim; whether the mint tokens' deployer balances (BIP, MOOM, MAI) came from emergencyWithdraw, sweeps or sales; contents of Telegram, Discord and Farcaster posts.


### Inferences
- Nobody built a durable "mint any token by paying through x402" product. The mint wave of 2025-10-19 to 2025-10-29 used one-off contracts that are all dormant, and the launch-as-a-service sites are small, pseudonymous or founder-led, or were side features of established companies (Heurist, Covalent) that went quiet within weeks to months. Only Regents Labs shows heavy 2026 engineering, and its x402 use is now a side feature.
- The x402 payment rail worked as designed; the weak point was custody. In every mint contract an operator-run minter decided who got tokens and an admin could withdraw the USDC, so buyers were trusting off-chain operators, which the "USDC in, token out" copy on PING's site did not say. Whether any withdrawal covered refunds or costs is unknown.
- Clanker is the default plumbing (it deploys the token); x402 is the payment gate in front of it. That makes an "x402 token launchpad" a thin layer, which explains the quick copycat spread and the quick decay.
- Serial launching is visible mostly at the wallet level (shared launch services), so wallet counts alone cannot support a claim about a team; the named-builder cases (APINow.fun, MiroShark, x420's repo owner) rest on public repos and dated launches.

### Gaps
- Who operated the minter and admin accounts of the mint contracts: only addresses are known (contract facts); no attempt was made to identify people. I checked the minter and admin roles in detail only for PING; for MOOM and MAI the deployer sent the `batchMint` calls I sampled; for BIP, x420DOG and 叉肆零贰 the minter account was not identified.
- Where the withdrawn USDC went after the first hop, and whether payers beyond a contract's cap were refunded from other accounts.
- The number of mints made through x402.team's mint contract (log queries were blocked on the public RPCs) and any Heurist admin withdrawals.
- Whether a paid call to Quick Intel's gateway actually launches a token (no paid call was made); whether any Clanker token was ever launched through SpeedRun and why it is paused.
- Off-Base tools (Hello 402, XFOUR, X402.Fun, 1ly, MNTx402) rest on listings and repositories, not on testing.
- Zora: no x402 mint integration found; this rests on a sibling researcher's search and the Zora CLI changelog, not on a Zora statement.

## 3. Triage of x402-branded tokens on Base (CoinGecko "x402 Ecosystem", CoinMarketCap, GeckoTerminal and DexScreener lists) not covered by another researcher

### Takeaway
CoinGecko's x402 Ecosystem category held 135 coins on 2026-10-11, 62 of them with a Base contract. After removing nine covered by other researchers, 53 remain: 47 tokens triaged individually and six large multi-chain tokens (LINK, EIGEN, PEAQ, MAGIC, ACU, SKL) whose Base copy is a secondary deployment. Most of the 47 are older AI-agent tokens carrying an x402 tag rather than tokens built for x402: 15 were created on Base before x402 launched (2025-05-06), 15 more between that launch and PING's deployment (2025-10-18), and 17 from PING's deployment onward (counting PING). My verdicts for the 47: 2 Serious builder (FLOCK, Neurobro), 20 Credible but small, 19 Speculative and 5 Likely abandoned, plus TRANSLATE, the token of a profiled product. Thirty of the 47 have a real product; only about 20 show any live or coded x402 surface, and apart from AInalyst's click-game endpoint (8.9M transactions, $2.74M, mostly Nov-Dec 2025) and AnySpend's facilitator, the measured x402 revenue of a triaged token's own service is under $1,000 each (Aubrai's roughly $764 lifetime is the highest I recorded). Separately, a GeckoTerminal search for x402- and 402-named pools found 130 on Base (127 tabulated in 3D), 96 of them Clanker deployments; 34 were created in October 2025, 23 in November 2025 and 42 in February 2026 (including 25 "x402claw"-style tokens in twelve days). Almost none has trading volume or any product I could find.

### Cited Findings
- Universe: CoinGecko category x402-ecosystem lists 135 coins on 2026-10-11 (62 with a Base contract, 73 without); CoinMarketCap's "x402 Ecosystem" list shows 38 tokens, and GeckoTerminal's "X402 Protocol" category page shows 20 pools ranked by 24-hour transactions, of which 13 are on Base (vAPI, BNKR, HTTPBASE20, MRDN, PING, WCHAN, HermesOS, SANTA, AIKEK, LUCA, GLORIA, AURA, AIN). [CoinGecko API](https://api.coingecko.com/api/v3/coins/markets?vs_currency=usd&category=x402-ecosystem&per_page=250&page=1); [CoinMarketCap](https://coinmarketcap.com/view/x402-ecosystem/); [GeckoTerminal](https://www.geckoterminal.com/category/x402-protocol) A sibling researcher's note lists the 73 non-Base members and the per-token x402 footprint in its section 1.3: [ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md)
- Covered by other researchers (not triaged here): BNKR (Bankr), OLAS (Olas), DREAMS (Daydreams), OTTO (Otto AI), HEU (Heurist), GLORIA (Gloria AI), MRDN (Meridian), AURA (AurraCloud), SANTA (Questflow). Established multi-chain tokens whose Base contract is a secondary deployment (the x402 tie is an integration or a CoinGecko tag, not an x402 token): LINK, EIGEN, PEAQ, MAGIC, ACU, SKL; FLOCK is multi-chain too but is profiled.
- Caveat on the age buckets: OpenServ's Base token (created 2025-05-19) is a Wormhole-wrapped copy of an Ethereum token created 2024-11-07, so counting its Ethereum origin makes 16 tokens older than x402; AlphaKEK's 2024-06-17 date is the earliest code at the address (no creation transaction found). [Base public RPC, queried 2026-10-11](https://basescan.org)
- Market-cap bands (liveness only): of the 135 category coins, 109 are under $1M and 68 under $100k; Chainlink ($9.85B) and EigenCloud ($0.24B) make up almost all of the $10.87B total. [CoinGecko API](https://api.coingecko.com/api/v3/coins/markets?vs_currency=usd&category=x402-ecosystem&per_page=250&page=1)
- Category membership is not usage. The sibling researcher's domain matching found no x402 footprint for OpenServ, FLOCK, Hive Intelligence, Cod3x, Sally A1C, ArAIstotle, ROBA, VPay, SMOL, Regent, Maya, HEIR, TRAI and others, and the profiles here confirm that for most of them; the matching missed live or coded x402 for Loky (api.dapplooker.com returns 402 responses), WachAI (a Bazaar-listed TokenSense API) and StarkBot (x402 in its original code), and the WachAI and MIO figures in that note are payee- or facilitator-level. Small but live seller footprints exist for Slinky Layer (3.2K transactions, $30 in 30 days), Aubrai, Capminal, MiroShark, WachAI and TRANSLATE; Elsa's x402 origin shows 5.2K transactions and $48 all-time, last 2026-02-02. [ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md section 1.3a](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md)

#### 3D. x402- and 402-named tokens on Base found by GeckoTerminal search (name-squats; first-pool date shown)
Method note: GeckoTerminal's search endpoint returns a limited list per query, so this is a lower bound. Launch dates are first-pool dates (equal to the token creation date for Clanker tokens). "Clanker v4/v3.1 (factory verified)" means the creation transaction's `to` address matched the Clanker factory; "Clanker (inferred from b07 suffix)" means the address ends in Clanker's vanity suffix "b07" but I did not look up the creation transaction (26 of the 96 Clanker rows were verified). Team is anonymous for all (only deployer wallets are visible). Real product and still-active are "no evidence" and "no" respectively because every pool had zero 24-hour volume (or under $10) when pulled and none appears in CoinGecko's category; I did not open a website for each. Monthly counts of x402/402-named pools found: 2025-05: 1; 2025-08: 1; 2025-10: 34; 2025-11: 23; 2025-12: 1; 2026-01: 9; 2026-02: 42; 2026-03: 3; 2026-04: 2; 2026-05: 5; 2026-06: 1; 2026-07: 1; 2026-08: 2; 2026-09: 1; 2026-10: 4. #### 3F. Full profiles for tokens attached to real products (not covered by another researcher)
Profiles are grouped by research batch; each is followed, at the end of its batch, by a short block of observations that cut across the batch. Each profile follows the requested headings. Where a profile quotes the sibling researcher's x402scan table, that is stated in the profile. Opinions are labelled as opinion. The PING profile is in section 1 and the launch-tool profiles are in section 2.

#### Profile: PRXVT / px402 (PRXVT by Virtuals)
**One-line:** Anonymous-operator privacy toolkit for AI agents: px402 (private x402 payments), a prxvt.ai LLM gateway, HushKit (encrypted agent messaging) and an ERC-8183 privacy hook, with a token launched on Virtuals. [prxvt.com](https://prxvt.com)

**x402 role:**
- v2 is a buyer-side privacy layer. The agent funds a USDC reserve once and draws blind-signed coins. A relayer pays the x402 merchant, so the merchant receives plain USDC and never sees the agent's address. [docs](https://docs.prxvt.com/getting-started/what-is-px402.md)
- It works with any Base-USDC x402 merchant through a bridge, or with merchants that name a px402 facilitator. The fee is 1% on top of each payment. [docs](https://docs.prxvt.com/getting-started/what-is-px402.md)
- The v1 SDK (Nov 2025) was a different design: zero-knowledge notes (Groth16). [sdk README](https://github.com/PRXVT/sdk)

**Chains:** v2 is Base only ([docs](https://docs.prxvt.com/getting-started/what-is-px402.md)). The v1 README lists Base and Polygon ([README](https://github.com/PRXVT/sdk)).

**Token:** PRXVT; Base; 0x4b5D32A07b8d3eC5D6928cAa30196f8dd6a7C5A9; fixed supply of 1,000,000,000. [docs](https://docs.prxvt.com/token/overview.md)
- Launch: the contract was created 2026-01-06 05:20:29 UTC by EOA 0xf96aa247.... It went through a shared launch-helper contract (0xc3538ddd..., created 2025-10-07) that took VIRTUAL, minted the supply and created a bonding pair. [creation tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x1f34c513536f9102a3fb8ad40ec26edd03a4cf6b574cc18c45b999c0bf6b3af7) Virtuals' own record shows factory BONDING_V2 and an empty genesis field, so it is a standard bonding-curve launch, not a Genesis sale; launch time 2026-01-07 06:00 UTC, pool created 07:39 UTC. [Virtuals API](https://api.virtuals.io/api/virtuals?filters%5BtokenAddress%5D%5B%24eqi%5D=0x4b5d32a07b8d3ec5d6928caa30196f8dd6a7c5a9&populate=*)
- Stated utility: all PRXVT product fees go to holders, plus governance; the docs do not say whether payout is automatic, staked or claimed. [utility](https://docs.prxvt.com/token/utility.md) Real work today: not shown. The v2 reserve held about 201 USDC on 2026-10-11 and no payout contract is named in the docs. [reserve](https://basescan.org/address/0xdd31c57E8F7CAC78090a516292f0C9Dea6c6119f)
- Distribution is told two ways. Docs: Community 50%, Team 25% (1-year lock plus 6-month vest), Capital Formation 25% locked. [supply](https://docs.prxvt.com/token/supply.md) Virtuals record: 250M team tokens locked to 2027-01-07, plus a 420M-token (42%) team initial buy paid with 197,980 VIRTUAL, locked 30 days then vested linearly over six months from 2026-02-05. [Virtuals API](https://api.virtuals.io/api/virtuals?filters%5BtokenAddress%5D%5B%24eqi%5D=0x4b5d32a07b8d3ec5d6928caa30196f8dd6a7c5a9&populate=*) The creator EOA held about 22.3M PRXVT (2.2%) on 2026-10-11; I did not trace where the rest went. [token](https://basescan.org/token/0x4b5D32A07b8d3eC5D6928cAa30196f8dd6a7C5A9)
- Liveness: still trading, about $228k pool liquidity. [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0x4b5d32a07b8d3ec5d6928caa30196f8dd6a7c5a9)

**Team:** Anonymous. Site, docs and Terms name no people and no legal entity; the Terms refer to unnamed operators and are dated 2026-06-17. [terms](https://prxvt.ai/terms) X @prxvtai and GitHub user PRXVT are both linked from prxvt.com (high confidence); Telegram t.me/PRXVTCommunity is linked from the docs (high). [site](https://prxvt.com), [docs](https://docs.prxvt.com/token/overview.md) The GitHub profile sets no X handle and shows no people. [GitHub API](https://api.github.com/users/PRXVT) Virtuals shows the creator as a Privy login ID. [Virtuals API](https://api.virtuals.io/api/virtuals?filters%5BtokenAddress%5D%5B%24eqi%5D=0x4b5d32a07b8d3ec5d6928caa30196f8dd6a7c5a9&populate=*) Contract fact only: EOA 0xa116959f... deployed the HushKit contracts on 2026-03-15 and the v2 reserve on 2026-10-05. [HushKit registry](https://basescan.org/address/0x6cd5534f2946f270C50C873f4E3f936735f128B4), [reserve](https://basescan.org/address/0xdd31c57E8F7CAC78090a516292f0C9Dea6c6119f)

**Track record and pivots:** 2025-10-25 GitHub user created; 2025-11-07 sdk repo; 2025-11-25 @prxvt/sdk 1.0.0 to 1.0.2 on npm. [repos](https://api.github.com/users/PRXVT/repos), [npm](https://registry.npmjs.org/@prxvt%2fsdk) 2026-01-06/07 Virtuals launch (px402 then described as an SDK and playground, mainnet to come, a 0.1-5% deposit fee). [Virtuals API](https://api.virtuals.io/api/virtuals?filters%5BtokenAddress%5D%5B%24eqi%5D=0x4b5d32a07b8d3ec5d6928caa30196f8dd6a7c5a9&populate=*) 2026-01-28 added to the x402 ecosystem partner list. [x402 PR #682](https://github.com/x402-foundation/x402/pull/682) 2026-03-13 to 03-23 ERC-8183 hook-contracts fork, HushKit (npm and Base contracts 03-15), ERC-8183-PRIVACY repo. [GitHub](https://github.com/PRXVT) 2026-10-05 v2 reserve deployed on Base and px402 2.0.0 published to npm (2.0.2 on 10-06). [npm](https://registry.npmjs.org/px402) v1 to v2 changed both the design (ZK notes on two chains to blind-signed coins on Base) and the fee (deposit fee to 1% per payment). [README](https://github.com/PRXVT/sdk), [docs](https://docs.prxvt.com/getting-started/what-is-px402.md)

**Trend-hopping score (1-5):** 2. Privacy for agent payments is constant from the first repo (Nov 2025) to v2 (Oct 2026), and x402 was the first product. The messaging, LLM-gateway and ERC-8183 pieces follow hot standards, but each has code or a live site. [GitHub](https://github.com/PRXVT), [prxvt.ai](https://prxvt.ai)

**Funding:** None found. The docs list a 25% Capital Formation bucket, locked, with no terms. [supply](https://docs.prxvt.com/token/supply.md)

**Product and traction:** The v2 reserve is a 23.5 KB contract holding about 201 USDC on 2026-10-11. [reserve](https://basescan.org/address/0xdd31c57E8F7CAC78090a516292f0C9Dea6c6119f) npm downloads in the 30 days to 2026-10-09: @prxvt/sdk 12,878 (no release since 2025-11-25), hushkit 17,630, px402 800 in its first week; bots and mirrors inflate these counts. [npm API](https://api.npmjs.org/downloads/point/last-month/@prxvt/sdk) GitHub stars: sdk 24, HushKit 7. [sdk](https://github.com/PRXVT/sdk), [HushKit](https://github.com/PRXVT/HushKit) Virtuals shows 4,909 holders. [Virtuals API](https://api.virtuals.io/api/virtuals?filters%5BtokenAddress%5D%5B%24eqi%5D=0x4b5d32a07b8d3ec5d6928caa30196f8dd6a7c5a9&populate=*) The sibling table shows no matched x402scan footprint for PRXVT, which is expected under v2 because the relayer, not PRXVT, appears as the payer to merchants (inference). [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com)

**Last six months (2026-04-11 to 2026-10-11):** commits per month (Apr 11-30 / May / Jun / Jul / Aug / Sep / Oct 1-11): sdk 0/0/0/0/0/0/0 (7 all-time), HushKit 0/0/0/0/0/0/0 (2), ERC-8183-PRIVACY 0/0/0/0/0/0/0 (1). [sdk](https://github.com/PRXVT/sdk), [HushKit](https://github.com/PRXVT/HushKit), [ERC-8183-PRIVACY](https://github.com/PRXVT/ERC-8183-PRIVACY) Shipped: the v2 reserve and npm package (Oct 5-6); a models.dev fork appeared on 2026-06-16, purpose not stated. [GitHub API](https://api.github.com/users/PRXVT/repos) The v2 source is not in the public repo and the npm package lists no repository. [npm](https://registry.npmjs.org/px402)

**Direction:** The site says a Blackbox component is in development and the product list keeps growing ([site](https://prxvt.com)); docs promise holders a share of fees from future products ([utility](https://docs.prxvt.com/token/utility.md)); the January Virtuals roadmap named multi-chain support and security audits, neither shown as done ([Virtuals API](https://api.virtuals.io/api/virtuals?filters%5BtokenAddress%5D%5B%24eqi%5D=0x4b5d32a07b8d3ec5d6928caa30196f8dd6a7c5a9&populate=*)).

**Competitors:** Opinion, not sourced: shielded-pool and stealth-address payment tools, and plain x402 with throwaway wallets. One adjacent package is private-Zcash-over-x402 ([CipherPay x402](https://github.com/atmospherelabs-dev/cipherpay-x402)).

**Green and red flags:** Green: shipped a mainnet v2 with consistent docs and npm; reserve described as no-owner and no-admin-withdrawal (project claim, not audited by me) [FAQ](https://docs.prxvt.com/resources/faq.md); MIT-licensed SDK and HushKit [GitHub](https://github.com/PRXVT/HushKit). Red: anonymous operators with no entity [terms](https://prxvt.ai/terms); no audit statement found for v2 [FAQ](https://docs.prxvt.com/resources/faq.md); v2 code not public [npm](https://registry.npmjs.org/px402); the site says v2 is coming soon while docs and npm show it live [site](https://prxvt.com); the contract address printed on prxvt.ai has no bytecode on Base or Polygon on 2026-10-11 [prxvt.ai](https://prxvt.ai); the two distribution accounts differ and the 42% team initial buy now sits mostly outside the creator wallet [Virtuals API](https://api.virtuals.io/api/virtuals?filters%5BtokenAddress%5D%5B%24eqi%5D=0x4b5d32a07b8d3ec5d6928caa30196f8dd6a7c5a9&populate=*); fee revenue unproven at about $201 of reserve [reserve](https://basescan.org/address/0xdd31c57E8F7CAC78090a516292f0C9Dea6c6119f).

**Not confirmed:** funding, team identity, any audit, where the 420M tokens went, whether a fee-payout contract exists, why the prxvt.ai address has no code, the purpose of the models.dev fork.

**Verdict:** Credible but small; momentum Accelerating (since 2026-10-05, after about six months of public silence). Opinion: the product is real and shipped on mainnet, and privacy is a coherent niche for x402. But it is anonymous, unaudited as far as I can see, tiny in usage, and the token story has loose ends. The 42% initial buy and the stale site are the first questions to put to the team.

**Tracking card:** site prxvt.com (also prxvt.ai, app.prxvt.com); docs docs.prxvt.com; GitHub github.com/PRXVT; X @prxvtai; Telegram t.me/PRXVTCommunity; Farcaster not found; people none; token 0x4b5D32A07b8d3eC5D6928cAa30196f8dd6a7C5A9; v2 reserve 0xdd31c57E8F7CAC78090a516292f0C9Dea6c6119f.

**Sources (accessed 2026-10-11):** https://prxvt.com · https://prxvt.ai · https://prxvt.ai/terms · https://docs.prxvt.com/token/overview.md · https://docs.prxvt.com/token/supply.md · https://docs.prxvt.com/token/utility.md · https://docs.prxvt.com/getting-started/what-is-px402.md · https://docs.prxvt.com/resources/faq.md · https://github.com/PRXVT · https://api.github.com/users/PRXVT · https://registry.npmjs.org/px402 · https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0x4b5d32a07b8d3ec5d6928caa30196f8dd6a7c5a9 · Virtuals API record (URL above) · https://github.com/x402-foundation/x402/pull/682 · sibling table lines 282-347 of ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md.

#### Profile: SlinkyLayer ($SLINKY)
**One-line:** Wallet-based private AI chat that routes through Tor and pays per request for third-party tools in x402 USDC on Base; other official channels describe it as an x402 API marketplace. [site](https://slinkylayer.ai), [Galxe](https://app.galxe.com/quest/m9VnsoALBFsJ9TLUvAsH4E)

**x402 role:** Payment rail for tool calls: the user approves an x402 USDC payment before a paid tool runs, with no subscription or prepaid balance. [docs](https://docs.slinky.network/start-here/using-slinkylayer.md) SlinkyLayer runs its own x402 gateway; its discovery document listed 20 proxied resources on 2026-10-11 (models, web search, research, market data, prediction markets, onchain data). [discovery](https://x402.slinkylayer.ai/.well-known/x402) The roadmap adds x402 USDC on Solana and third-party x402 tools in Q4 2026. [roadmap](https://docs.slinky.network/slinkylayer/slinkylayer-roadmap.md)

**Chains:** Base for USDC payments and the token ([tokenomics](https://docs.slinky.network/slinkylayer/slinky-tokenomics.md)); Solana planned Q4 2026 ([roadmap](https://docs.slinky.network/slinkylayer/slinkylayer-roadmap.md)); Tor for outbound routing ([docs](https://docs.slinky.network/core-concepts/the-slinkylayer-tool-network.md)).

**Token:** SLINKY; Base; 0xbb3d6c47379abb4713acffffa27483b726ee67db; 1B supply.
- Launch: plain deploy by EOA 0x8da16359... on 2026-08-17 07:05:45 UTC with the full supply minted to the deployer in that transaction; no factory, so not Clanker or a launchpad. [creation tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x2c3e272f0d039d2dc0c808e17478e1cd57e9c1f9fa97072a78b03f7212c5d495) The bytecode has no mint, burn, owner or pause functions. [token](https://basescan.org/token/0xbb3d6c47379abb4713acffffa27483b726ee67db) Aerodrome pool created 2026-08-18 10:33:03 UTC. [pool](https://basescan.org/address/0xbA585d3Fff2626BF77bDf0d8407BD4E0befB97C9)
- Allocation: 65% community/ecosystem in treasury, 15.8% liquidity, 15% team (6-month cliff, 18-month vest), 1.9% pre-seed, 1.3% seed, 1% advisors; no public sale. [tokenomics](https://docs.slinky.network/slinkylayer/slinky-tokenomics.md) The treasury is a Safe v1.4.1 (2-of-3) created 2026-08-16 that still held exactly 650,000,000 SLINKY on 2026-10-11. [Safe](https://basescan.org/address/0x8d9694251c0bE8C5aFd475Eff8b7af0B6c52EFa6)
- Utility: access token for private-chat bandwidth, held not spent; tools are paid in USDC, not SLINKY; holding thresholds are published in the app. [token docs](https://docs.slinky.network/slinkylayer/slinky-token.md) Real work in the product: unconfirmed (the app is script-rendered and needs a wallet). [app](https://app.slinkylayer.ai) The Terms dated 2025-10-14 already mention SLINKY as a reward token. [Terms](https://docs.slinky.network/legal/terms-of-service.md)
- Liveness: still trading, about $99k pool liquidity. [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0xbb3d6c47379abb4713acffffa27483b726ee67db)

**Team:** Not found. No names, entity or jurisdiction on the site or in the docs; the Terms (effective 2025-10-14) are governed by BVI law. [Terms](https://docs.slinky.network/legal/terms-of-service.md) Official channels, from the Linktree the site links (high confidence): X @slinkylayer, Telegram t.me/slinkylayer, a Discord invite, and a LinkedIn company page. [Linktree](https://linktr.ee/slinkylayer) No GitHub org: github.com/SlinkyLayer returns 404 and a GitHub search found only third-party repos. [GitHub](https://github.com/SlinkyLayer)

**Track record and pivots:** 2025-10-14 Terms and Privacy effective. [Terms](https://docs.slinky.network/legal/terms-of-service.md) 2026-01-23 listed in the x402 ecosystem repo as an open market for APIs with ERC-8004 reputation [PR #618](https://github.com/x402-foundation/x402/pull/618); the Galxe page uses the same API-market pitch and cites a hackathon grand prize (unverified) [Galxe](https://app.galxe.com/quest/m9VnsoALBFsJ9TLUvAsH4E). Q2 2026, per the roadmap and self-reported as done: dApp beta, Tor routing, Nemotron model, x402 tools, gasless USDC payments. [roadmap](https://docs.slinky.network/slinkylayer/slinkylayer-roadmap.md) 2026-08-16 to 08-18 treasury Safe, token and pool. [token](https://basescan.org/token/0xbb3d6c47379abb4713acffffa27483b726ee67db) Current site and docs describe a private access layer for AI; API-market and ERC-8004 language is absent from the docs. [docs](https://docs.slinky.network/start-here/introduction-to-slinkylayer.md) A third-party list dated 2026-03-11 called it an x402 scaling layer (low reliability). [list](https://github.com/Jack0814/agent-payment)

**Trend-hopping score (1-5):** 3. The x402 core is constant since at least Oct 2025, but the public pitch moved from API marketplace to private AI chat, with Tor, a token and rewards added in 2026. Each phase has a working surface (20 listed resources, an app). [discovery](https://x402.slinkylayer.ai/.well-known/x402), [docs](https://docs.slinky.network/start-here/introduction-to-slinkylayer.md)

**Funding:** The site shows backer logos for Animoca Brands, Draper Dragon, Cogitent Ventures, CSP DAO and Levitate Labs [site](https://slinkylayer.ai); Galxe names three of them [Galxe](https://app.galxe.com/quest/m9VnsoALBFsJ9TLUvAsH4E). Tokenomics show pre-seed and seed rounds (3.2% of supply combined) but no amounts, dates or leads. [tokenomics](https://docs.slinky.network/slinkylayer/slinky-tokenomics.md)

**Product and traction:** x402scan, Base: all-time 23,927 transactions, $1,087, 4,056 payers; last 30 days 3,181 transactions, $30.29, 3,144 payers (about one cent each); origins x402.slinkylayer.ai and dev-api2.slinkylayer.ai (a dev host); last payment 2026-10-11. [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com) The sibling table gives the same totals (3.2K / $30; 23.9K / $1.1K). Galxe shows 48,159 followers and a quest page; whether the quest drives the payments is not established. [Galxe](https://app.galxe.com/quest/m9VnsoALBFsJ9TLUvAsH4E) The sub-agent's unauthenticated GET/POST to the listed resources returned 404 (no 402 challenge seen); docs say route IDs rotate. [Beta status](https://docs.slinky.network/start-here/public-beta-status.md)

**Last six months (2026-04-11 to 2026-10-11):** no public GitHub, so no commit counts. Per the roadmap the Q2 2026 beta is done [roadmap](https://docs.slinky.network/slinkylayer/slinkylayer-roadmap.md); token, treasury Safe and pool are on chain (Aug 2026) [Safe](https://basescan.org/address/0x8d9694251c0bE8C5aFd475Eff8b7af0B6c52EFa6).

**Direction:** Q3 2026 bandwidth system, rewards program, credits dashboard; Q4 2026 Solana x402, third-party x402 tools, staking, model routing; Q1 2027 TEE models, Chrome extension, mobile app (timing may change). [roadmap](https://docs.slinky.network/slinkylayer/slinkylayer-roadmap.md)

**Competitors:** Opinion, not sourced: private or no-logs AI gateways and other x402 tool aggregators; Venice also appears in the x402 ecosystem pull.

**Green and red flags:** Green: a 2-of-3 treasury Safe that verifiably holds the allocation [Safe](https://basescan.org/address/0x8d9694251c0bE8C5aFd475Eff8b7af0B6c52EFa6); a plain ERC-20 with no mint or owner [token](https://basescan.org/token/0xbb3d6c47379abb4713acffffa27483b726ee67db); no public sale [tokenomics](https://docs.slinky.network/slinkylayer/slinky-tokenomics.md); candid docs about what third-party tools see; a live x402 gateway with 20 resources [discovery](https://x402.slinkylayer.ai/.well-known/x402). Red: no named team, entity or GitHub [Terms](https://docs.slinky.network/legal/terms-of-service.md); a dev host among the x402scan origins [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com); about one cent per payer suggests campaign-driven usage (inference); unverified hackathon claim [Linktree](https://linktr.ee/slinkylayer); two self-descriptions (API market and private chat) live in different official places [Galxe](https://app.galxe.com/quest/m9VnsoALBFsJ9TLUvAsH4E), [docs](https://docs.slinky.network/start-here/introduction-to-slinkylayer.md).

**Not confirmed:** team, entity, round sizes and investors; the hackathon win; whether SLINKY gating works in the app; what sat behind the 2025 API-market phase; why the listed gateway routes returned 404.

**Verdict:** Credible but small; momentum Steady. Opinion: a real gateway and a clean token contract, but an anonymous team and thin organic usage. Watch whether the Q4 roadmap items (Solana, staking) ship on time.

#### Profile: vAPI Network ($VAPI)
**One-line:** Base-based payment and trust layer for AI agents. It has an open-source wallet/CLI/MCP client that pays x402 APIs and a "Call" directory that is launching; Tasks (an escrowed task market) is in preview and Router, Stake and Agents are coming soon. [site](https://vapinetwork.ai), [status](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/products/roadmap-status.md)

**x402 role:** Buyer side. The client keeps keys locally, applies spend caps before signing, pays exact USDC to x402 APIs, and tags each payment with builder-code `vapi`. [README](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/vAPI-Network/vapi-network/main/README.md) Call lists self-listed APIs (a 5% network fee through an immutable splitter), partner listings, and external listings mirrored from the Coinbase x402 Bazaar. [Call docs](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/call/readme.md)

**Chains:** Base for the token and payments; the client also pays on Arc mainnet ([networks](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/call/reference/networks-and-schemes.md)); Tasks contracts exist only on Base Sepolia ([contracts](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/tasks/contracts.md)).

**Token:** vAPI; Base; 0x1eA8deB921AA6015f4938010C848140b98108Dd2.
- Launch: plain deploy by EOA 0x4cc2aa94... on 2026-08-31 05:02:15 UTC with 100M minted, no factory; the Uniswap v4 pool appeared about 13 minutes later (05:15 UTC). [creation tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x3b43fe93b133a1e489f02ad48627ebfdbef1bde60a9ff0a93859a40c5669c236), [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0x1ea8deb921aa6015f4938010c848140b98108dd2) Docs describe a 3% pool fee taken in vAPI and sent to the treasury via a fee hook; the hook address could not be confirmed on chain (log queries were rate-limited). [token docs](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/token-and-foundation/vapi-token.md)
- Supply: bytecode has burn but no mint, owner or pause. [token](https://basescan.org/token/0x1eA8deB921AA6015f4938010C848140b98108Dd2) On-chain supply on 2026-10-11 was 66,261,232, so 33.74% of the initial 100M is burned; the About page gave the same figure for 2026-10-04; docs do not explain the burn and it was not traced. [About](https://vapinetwork.ai/about)
- Allocation: LP 80%, ecosystem 7%, founding team 6%, strategic sale 5% (two tranches, weighted $2M FDV), airdrop 2%; team cliff is 3 months in docs and 14 days on the About page. [docs](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/token-and-foundation/vapi-token.md), [About](https://vapinetwork.ai/about)
- Utility: staking for a daily Router allowance and reviewer eligibility, plus buyback-and-burn from fees; staking and governance are not live, so the token does no product work today. [network economics](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/token-and-foundation/network-economics.md)
- Liveness: actively traded, about $415k pool liquidity and about 500 trades in 24h. [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0x1ea8deb921aa6015f4938010c848140b98108dd2)

**Team:** Pseudonymous. The About page names Vertical Labs, an independent company, as leading core development, plus three first-name leads: Mark (protocol), Mac (technical) and Laurens (strategy), with X links on the page @MarkTbuilds, @asyncmac and @laurensvanduin (high confidence for handle and role; no surnames or GitHub accounts published; same-name GitHub lookups returned 404). [About](https://vapinetwork.ai/about) Legal: docs name Echelon Finance Ltd (Marshall Islands, reg. 127117) as operator until the vAPI Foundation, being incorporated in Panama, exists [entity](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/legal/entity.md); the site Terms dated 2026-08-06 and 2026-08-31 already call the Foundation the publisher [Terms](https://vapinetwork.ai/terms). All 9 GitHub commits come from one shared account, vapi-development; the org location field says Panama. [org](https://github.com/vAPI-Network) Project X is @vAPI_Network (GitHub org field and site). [GitHub API](https://api.github.com/orgs/vAPI-Network)

**Track record and pivots:** 2026-07-07 GitHub org created; the Hacken report slug dates to Jul 2026 and covers the token and fee hook only. [org](https://github.com/vAPI-Network), [audits](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/protocol/audits.md) 2026-08-31 token live. 2026-09-08 FeeRouter, FeeSplitterFactory and BuybackBurner deployed by EOA 0xf6997435...; the Call fee contracts are unaudited per docs, and the Base Sepolia Tasks council address is the same EOA. [FeeRouter](https://basescan.org/address/0xe11aeeC31E54E8E6E66382c8BdBFfd1000E0194B), [audits](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/protocol/audits.md) 2026-09-10 first npm publish, 9 releases through 0.8.0 on 2026-10-01. [eggs](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/token-and-foundation/genesis-eggs.md) 2026-10-02 public repo created. [About](https://vapinetwork.ai/about), [entity](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/legal/entity.md)

**Trend-hopping score (1-5):** 4. A new entity (July 2026) stacks the current narratives at once: x402, ERC-8004, MCP, A2A, agent wallets, a task market, an inference router and an NFT. The token launched before Call, Router, Stake and Agents. There is real code and an audit of the token, so not a 5. **Funding:** The strategic sale (5%, two tranches, weighted $2M FDV) names no investors; docs say Echelon Finance Ltd funds development; the console page claims an 85,501 USDC reward pool held by the Foundation with no address given (unverified). [token docs](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/token-and-foundation/vapi-token.md), [foundation](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/token-and-foundation/foundation.md), [console](https://api.vapinetwork.ai/call)

**Product and traction:** The open-source client is live: 0.8.0 on npm, 148 downloads in the last week and 1,662 in the last month. [npm API](https://api.npmjs.org/downloads/point/last-month/vapi-network) The hosted Call console says coming soon and /api/call/stats returned 404 on 2026-10-11. [console](https://api.vapinetwork.ai/call) The only vAPI x402 service in the Bazaar pull for this note is a decoder on a staging host (api-staging.vapinetwork.ai): 12 calls and 3 payers in 30 days, last call 2026-10-06 (the sibling table gives the same 1 resource / 12 calls). [Bazaar](https://api.cdp.coinbase.com/platform/v2/x402/discovery/resources)

**Last six months (2026-04-11 to 2026-10-11):** vapi-network commits per month (Apr 11-30 / May / Jun / Jul / Aug / Sep / Oct 1-11): 0/0/0/0/0/0/9, all by one account. [repo](https://github.com/vAPI-Network/vapi-network) npm releases: 0.2.0 (09-18), 0.3.0 (09-19), 0.5.0 (09-25), 0.7.0 (09-30), 0.8.0 (10-01). [npm](https://registry.npmjs.org/vapi-network)

**Direction:** Call launch; Router, Stake and Agents, with staking gated on a third-party audit; Tasks to mainnet (testnet only today); owner rules for agents, a provider trust score, pay-per-usage, an A2A card, paid MCP tools; later batched micropayments and publishing the trust score to ERC-8004. [status](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/products/roadmap-status.md)

**Competitors:** Opinion, not sourced: Coinbase's x402 Bazaar (vAPI mirrors it), other agent-wallet clients and x402 facilitators. [Call docs](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/call/readme.md)

**Green and red flags:** Green: a token audited by Hacken; clean bytecode (no mint, owner or pause) [token](https://basescan.org/token/0x1eA8deB921AA6015f4938010C848140b98108Dd2); a working open-source client; a disclaimer that it is unrelated to Vapi.ai [README](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/vAPI-Network/vapi-network/main/README.md). Red: the homepage says "Hacken audits all public vAPI smart contracts" but the docs say the Call fee contracts launched unaudited and Hacken covered only the token and hook [home](https://vapinetwork.ai), [audits](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/protocol/audits.md); a 33.74% burn with no stated cause [About](https://vapinetwork.ai/about); token live before products; two legal framings (Foundation versus Echelon) [entity](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/legal/entity.md), [Terms](https://vapinetwork.ai/terms); two team-cliff figures [About](https://vapinetwork.ai/about); an NFT mint with no promised utility [eggs](https://docs.vapinetwork.ai/token-and-foundation/genesis-eggs.md); a staging host in the Bazaar [Bazaar](https://api.cdp.coinbase.com/platform/v2/x402/discovery/resources).

**Not confirmed:** team surnames and earlier projects, and who Vertical Labs and Echelon are beyond these pages; Hacken report contents (the page returned 403); the burn cause and the v4 hook address; the 85,501 USDC pool; strategic-sale investors.

**Verdict:** Speculative; momentum Accelerating. Opinion: strong shipping speed on an open client, but three months old, with a token-first launch, products mostly unreleased, an unclear operator structure, and homepage audit wording that overstates the docs. Look for whether Call actually launches and the burn is explained.

#### Profile: AnySpend ($ANY, B3 Labs)
**One-line:** B3 Labs' payments product: cross-chain swaps, fiat onramp, payment links and checkout across 90+ chains, plus an x402 facilitator where buyers pay with any EIP-2612/3009 token and sellers receive USDC. [site](https://anyspend.com), [docs](https://docs.anyspend.com/anyspend/x402-overview)

**x402 role:** Facilitator plus server and client SDKs: the client signs for its own token, AnySpend swaps to USDC and settles to the seller, and the buyer pays no gas. [overview](https://docs.anyspend.com/anyspend/x402-overview) The endpoint is mainnet.anyspend.com/x402; its live /supported list on 2026-10-11 held 9 networks, all x402 v1 scheme exact: Abstract, Arbitrum, Avalanche, Base, BNB Chain, Ethereum, Optimism, Polygon and Solana (docs claim more: 12 EVM mainnets including B3, IoTeX, Peaq and Sei). [supported](https://mainnet.anyspend.com/x402/supported), [facilitator doc](https://docs.anyspend.com/anyspend/x402-facilitator) It also runs an x402 ad and article marketplace paid in Base USDC. [ads](https://docs.anyspend.com/anyspend/marketplace-ads) Cross-chain payments add about 0.5% and take 2-5 minutes; no same-chain fee is stated. [network support](https://docs.anyspend.com/anyspend/x402-network-support)

**Chains:** Base (token and x402), other EVM chains and Solana via the facilitator; the site claims 90+ chains. [site](https://anyspend.com)

**Token:** ANY (the docs write $ANY); Base; 0xC17dda248E2D50fC006d8fEbb5A406dd31972712; 1B supply.
- Launch: created 2025-10-09 13:13:31 UTC through the standard CREATE2 deployer by EOA 0x0541d092...; the full supply was minted to a fresh EOA that holds none now. [creation tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x4a607a05927a56bbd1e6cde27ead646d03ac897e63f2b0f6ce6efbab7771a90d) Aerodrome WETH pool created 2025-10-10 04:37:53 UTC. [pool](https://basescan.org/address/0x4e6a3F08B1266cff9C52DBE5Cc4e7fF48f8bb055)
- Admin: the admin role is held by a 2-of-3 Safe (v1.4.1); the bytecode includes role-gated mint and burn functions (who holds the minter role was not checked). [Safe](https://basescan.org/address/0x803c99ce3cfc1db093a146000cbc2bc0c85099b6)
- Stated design: 90% to community and users, 10% to the AnySpend Foundation, none to team or investors; 95% of protocol revenue to buyback-and-burn; staking rebates; fee discounts at 100K, 500K and 1M ANY; governance. [tokenomics](https://docs.anyspend.com/anyspend/tokenomics) Season 1 snapshot was the week of 2025-10-06; Season 2 ran 90 days from 2025-10-10, earned by using AnySpend and B3 products. [airdrop](https://docs.anyspend.com/anyspend/airdrop)
- Real work: discounts and rebates were not tested. Total supply was still exactly 1,000,000,000 on 2026-10-11 with nothing at the zero or dead addresses, so no burn is visible on chain. [token](https://basescan.org/token/0xC17dda248E2D50fC006d8fEbb5A406dd31972712)
- Liveness: about $126k pool liquidity and near-zero volume (about $2 in 24h). [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0xc17dda248e2d50fc006d8febb5a406dd31972712)

**Team:** AnySpend publishes no separate team. Its parent B3 lists Daryl Xu (co-founder, CEO), Viktoriya Hying (co-founder, COO) and Sean Geng (co-founder, CTO) and says it was founded by former Coinbase leaders; no personal handles are linked. The footer names NPC Labs, reg. 299884 (jurisdiction not stated). [b3.fun](https://b3.fun) Handles: AnySpend X @anyspend (site meta tags and docs link; high); B3 X @b3labs and LinkedIn b3-labs-inc from b3.fun icon links (high); Discord discord.gg/b3dotfun from AnySpend docs. [docs](https://docs.anyspend.com), [b3.fun](https://b3.fun) GitHub org b3-fun, created 2024-04-27. [GitHub](https://github.com/b3-fun)

[npm](https://registry.npmjs.org/@b3dotfun%2fsdk)

**Direction:** AnySpend V2 as a DeFi automation engine (undated), the Platform API, payment links and the x402 ad marketplace. [tokenomics](https://docs.anyspend.com/anyspend/tokenomics), [ads](https://docs.anyspend.com/anyspend/marketplace-ads)

**Competitors:** AnySpend's docs say its facilitator integrates with Coinbase CDP x402 and PayAI ([facilitator doc](https://docs.anyspend.com/anyspend/x402-facilitator)). Opinion, not sourced: other multi-chain facilitators and cross-chain checkout widgets.

**Green and red flags:** Green: a named parent company with large backers [b3.fun](https://b3.fun); a working facilitator endpoint [supported](https://mainnet.anyspend.com/x402/supported); token admin is a multisig [Safe](https://basescan.org/address/0x803c99ce3cfc1db093a146000cbc2bc0c85099b6); maintained docs and SDK [docs](https://docs.anyspend.com). Red: the x402 line looks idle (last settlement 2026-08-17, last x402 npm release 2026-02-04) [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com), [npm](https://registry.npmjs.org/@b3dotfun%2fanyspend-x402); throughput concentrated in one origin; docs list more networks than /supported returns [facilitator doc](https://docs.anyspend.com/anyspend/x402-facilitator); the token has a mint function and the burn claim is not visible on chain [token](https://basescan.org/token/0xC17dda248E2D50fC006d8fEbb5A406dd31972712); the claimed Caldera metalayer routing is absent from the docs [docs index](https://docs.anyspend.com/llms.txt).

**Not confirmed:** funding amounts; the Caldera claim; an official launch announcement for $ANY (X was blocked to the fetcher); who holds the minter role; whether x402.anyspend.com is internal traffic; where the 1B went after minting.

**Verdict:** Credible but small; momentum Slowing on the x402 side. Opinion: the best-resourced name in this group, with named founders, large backers and a working product. But its x402 usage is one origin and has been dormant for about two months, and the SDK cadence has dropped sharply since spring.

#### Profile: AlphaKEK (AIKEK)
**One-line:** Named-team AI research company (Fractal Labs LLC). It launched an AI crypto-analytics token in 2023 and now sells an agent-evolution platform of reinforcement-learning environments, with a paid x402 API and an ERC-8004 agent. [alphakek.ai](https://alphakek.ai)

**x402 role:** Seller. api.alphakek.ai offers three fixed-price x402 v2 routes: ultrafast $0.012, fast $0.07, deep $0.348; unauthenticated POSTs returned HTTP 402 on 2026-10-11, payable in Base USDC (and a Global Dollar token on a second chain the project labels robinhood-chain). [OpenAPI](https://api.alphakek.ai/openapi.json), [agent info](https://api.alphakek.ai/erc8004/agent/info) The gateway first shipped between 2025-09-13 and 2025-11-14. [Dev Update #25](https://blog.alphakek.ai/dev-update-25/) The docs page for it points to a path that now 404s and the old single-price route returns 410, directing clients to the three tier routes. [docs](https://docs.alphakek.ai/developers/x402.md) The 2026-05-25 Memetic Arena post says each project Environment includes an x402 endpoint. [blog](https://blog.alphakek.ai/introducing-aikek-memetic-arena/)

**Chains:** Ethereum (main token), Base (bridged), Solana (Wormhole, 2024-11-17); x402 payments on Base. [tokenomics](https://docs.alphakek.ai/tokenomics.md), [Solana post](https://blog.alphakek.ai/solana-wormhole-is-live/)

**Token:** AIKEK; Ethereum 0x8dce83eca4af45dbe618da1779f9aaca43201084; Base 0x681a09a902d9c7445b3b1ab282c38d60c72f1f09; max supply 256M with 18.68M burned per docs. [tokenomics](https://docs.alphakek.ai/tokenomics.md)
- Launch: the Base token is not a separate launch. It was created 2024-06-17 19:47:25 UTC through the OP Stack standard token factory by EOA 0xdf63a5df..., mapped to the Ethereum token. [creation tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0xcd10da78766778d85fc7f15d5321f449abf048c877646a891535f21044901b9d) The same EOA is the x402 payTo address and agent wallet in the project's own API files, so the project made its own Base token. [agent info](https://api.alphakek.ai/erc8004/agent/info) Ethereum's first Uniswap pool dates to 2023-08-18; the Base v3 pool dates to 2024-08-17. [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0x8dce83eca4af45dbe618da1779f9aaca43201084) Base holds only 10.8M tokens (about 4% of max supply). [token](https://basescan.org/token/0x681a09a902d9c7445b3b1ab282c38d60c72f1f09)
- Utility: hold-to-access tiers, hold-to-build, hold-to-compute and DAO votes. Ethereum carries a 1% trade fee, 33% of it to buybacks and liquidity; Base has none. A Nov 2025 post says a trade fee buys back AIKEK. [tokenomics](https://docs.alphakek.ai/tokenomics.md), [Alive](https://blog.alphakek.ai/alive/) Tier gating was not tested. Liveness: the Base pool is nearly idle (about $12k liquidity, about $112 volume in 24h). [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0x681a09a902d9c7445b3b1ab282c38d60c72f1f09)

**Team:** Doxxed. The official site names Vladimir Sotnikov (founder and lead of AI; X @vvsotnikov), Vinny Le (co-founder and lead of BD; X @VinnyCorp), Valery Sotnikov (co-founder and ML engineer), Vladimir Khomenok (dev) and Jason Hatton (marketing; X @zeerowtax); the operator is Fractal Labs LLC. Handles come from links on the site (high confidence). [alphakek.ai](https://alphakek.ai) The founder's published background is JetBrains Research development lead, an ACM SIGSOFT paper award (2022) and GTC talks. [blog](https://blog.alphakek.ai/about-vladimir-sotnikov/) GitHub org alphakek-ai (created 2022-12-16) is linked from the official docs; the org itself sets no site or X. Each phase had a shipped product. [blog](https://blog.alphakek.ai), [agent info](https://api.alphakek.ai/erc8004/agent/info)

Red: docs are stale (they still describe the old product and a dead x402 path) [docs](https://docs.alphakek.ai/developers/x402.md); frequent narrative changes [blog](https://blog.alphakek.ai); the Base token is a small bridged slice with an idle pool [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0x681a09a902d9c7445b3b1ab282c38d60c72f1f09); no settled x402 payments seen on x402scan; much of the strategy is self-described (the NVIDIA recognition claim, the "bigger than all previous" Alive post) [Alive](https://blog.alphakek.ai/alive/).

**Not confirmed:** funding; the exact Ethereum token creation date (the first pool date was used); Fractal Labs LLC's jurisdiction; real x402 usage; tier gating; the NVIDIA claim.

**Verdict:** Credible but small; momentum Steady. Opinion: on the evidence, the closest of the five in this group to a serious builder, with a doxxed team and steady output. But the scale is unverified, the narrative moves often, and x402 is a side product. Check whether the Environments launched in 2026 attract real paying users.
