#### Observations across these profiles (PRXVT, SlinkyLayer, vAPI Network, AnySpend, AlphaKEK)
- x402 listings clustered after the 2025-10-25 CoinGecko category: PRXVT's GitHub account was created the same day (2025-10-25), the AnySpend x402 npm package followed on 2025-10-31, Slinky (2026-01-23) and PRXVT (2026-01-28) were added to the x402 ecosystem list within five days of each other, AlphaKEK's gateway shipped by 2025-11-14; vAPI is the late one (GitHub org 2026-07-07).
- SLINKY (2026-08-17) and vAPI (2026-08-31) were both plain deployments with custom contracts (no launchpad), two weeks apart, both with docs-first, roadmap-heavy presentation.
- ERC-8004 appears in three of five (Slinky's listing text, vAPI's docs and roadmap, AlphaKEK's agent id 9765); PRXVT forked ERC-8183 hook contracts in March 2026. Two of five (PRXVT, SlinkyLayer) are privacy-for-agents plays using x402 as the payment layer.
- Token before product: vAPI's token is live while Call is launching and Router, Stake and Agents are coming soon; PRXVT's token launched 2026-01-07 and px402 reached mainnet 2026-10-05; ANY launched with the product already running.
- No shared deployer addresses across the five projects.
- Docs/site drift: PRXVT's site says v2 coming soon while docs say live; vAPI's homepage and docs disagree on the audit and team cliff; AlphaKEK's docs carry a dead x402 path; AnySpend's docs list more networks than /supported returns.
- Not confirmed because WebSearch was unavailable: team identities for PRXVT, SlinkyLayer and vAPI (including Vertical Labs and Echelon Finance Ltd; X profile pages were blocked); funding amounts and investors for all five; independent coverage; the Babylon hackathon claim, AnySpend's Caldera routing claim and AlphaKEK's NVIDIA claim; Hacken's report contents (403); vAPI's Uniswap v4 hook address and burn cause; where PRXVT's 420M initial-buy tokens went.

#### Profile: OpenServ ($SERV)
**One-line:** Agent-infrastructure company that began as a multi-agent platform and TypeScript SDK (2024). Its homepage, dated 2026-10-10, now leads with "SERV Reasoning", an OpenAI/Anthropic-compatible API for enterprises and governments. It also offers a no-code builder, a token launchpad and an "x402 Marketplace". [Product page](https://www.openserv.ai/product)

**x402 role:** One feature among several, not the product.
- In the client and SDK, x402 is a trigger type next to webhook, cron, manual and Telegram: a workflow or agent can sit behind a pay-per-run paywall paid in USDC or ETH, and `discoverServices()` finds other paid agents. [Docs](https://docs.openserv.ai/llms-full.txt)
- The "Ideaboard" asks agents to ship an "x402 payable" URL; its public API on 2026-10-11 showed 331 ideas, 30 shipped, 15 agent accounts, and the newest idea created 2026-07-26. [Ideaboard API](https://api.launch.openserv.ai/ideas), [skill file](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/openserv-labs/skills/main/skills/openserv-ideaboard-api/SKILL.md)
- The homepage mentions x402 only as a nav link to platform.openserv.ai/x402-services, a JavaScript app that could not be read. [Homepage](https://openserv.ai) The earliest dated x402 item found is the docs changelog entry of 2026-06-26, "x402 marketplace agents". [Changelog](https://docs.openserv.ai/changelog/overview.md)
- x402scan footprint: none matched by domain. [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md) Judgement: a real code path with thin use.

**Chains:** Ethereum (original SERV). Base has a wrapped SERV, ERC-8004 identity NFTs minted on Base mainnet, and agent token launches paired on Aerodrome. An "OpenServ Layer 3" is described as planned. [Token docs](https://docs.openserv.ai/what-is-serv/the-serv-token.md), [ERC-8004 docs](https://docs.openserv.ai/vibecode/openclaw/erc8004-identity.md), [agent launches](https://docs.openserv.ai/launch/agent-launches.md)

**Token:** SERV; Ethereum 0x40e3d1A4B2C47d9AA61261F5606136ef73E28042; Base 0x5576d6ed9181f2225aff5282ac0ed29f755437ea. [Docs](https://docs.openserv.ai/what-is-serv/the-serv-token.md)
- Ethereum launch: a plain ERC-20 deployed 2024-11-07 22:36:47 UTC by 0x8dB4ab33B7091d458F068A23A0Be88cF6fCd00F6 (Blockscout tags it "OpenServ: Deployer"); total supply 1,000,000,000; a Uniswap v2 SERV/WETH pair was created the same minute. Method: self-deployed token plus immediate pool, not a launchpad mint. Launch allocation and vesting: not found. [Blockscout tx](https://eth.blockscout.com/api/v2/transactions/0x6000df27ffaaaef4f3a6aaf561333b8b7995b1eaf9e12aa77cb0c1acb43bd243), [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0x40e3d1A4B2C47d9AA61261F5606136ef73E28042)
- Base token: created 2025-05-19 09:29:57 UTC by a call from that same deployer address to 0x8d2de8d2f73f1f4cab472ac9a881c9b123c79627; the selector 0xe8059810 is `createWrapped(bytes)` and the proxy implementation contains "TokenBridge" strings. Inference (high confidence): the Base SERV is a Wormhole-style bridged copy, not a Base-native launch (Base supply is 92.7M against 1B on Ethereum), although CoinGecko tags SERV "Base Native". [Base tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x5a57f4432cb8c2bf6e51cb30b7b28f7d1bcaa1ece92a3e28346ba45196748908), [Base RPC](https://mainnet.base.org)
- Stated utility: a 5,000 SERV launch fee, credits for Build tools and the Reasoning API, future Layer 3 gas and governance; 25% of revenue from four named sources goes to market-buy and burn; staking is "coming" and on 2026-10-02 its rewards were re-tied to tokenized startups. [Token docs](https://docs.openserv.ai/what-is-serv/the-serv-token.md), [staking](https://docs.openserv.ai/launch/staking.md), [changelog](https://docs.openserv.ai/changelog/overview.md) Real work today: partly; the roadmap lists "$SERV integration as payment currency" and staking as Q4 2026 items; on-chain burns were not checked. [Homepage](https://openserv.ai)
- Liveness: still trading; the Aerodrome SERV/WETH pool holds about USD 0.85M. [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0x5576d6ed9181f2225aff5282ac0ed29f755437ea)

**Team:** Doxxed, with LinkedIn links on the team page (no people's X handles linked there): Tim Hafner (Founder and CEO), Lucas Hafner (Cofounder), Armagan Amcalar (CTO); also listed Dr. Eyup Cinar (AI Research Partner), Andres Korin (CFO, ex-JP Morgan), Murat Erkin Cicek (Senior Engineer), Edoardo Di M. (Marketing Lead, ex-Galxe), Greg Ivanov (Strategic Advisor, ex-Google BD), Hassan Raza (Head of Data Security), Gea Eman (Chief of Staff). Company X @openservai and Telegram t.me/openservai are linked from the team page. [Team page](https://www.openserv.ai/team) GitHub user `dashersw` has the profile name "Armagan Amcalar", self-declares X @dashersw and is the top SDK contributor (61 commits); the match is by name and role (confidence high); OpenServ's pages do not link it. [GitHub profile](https://api.github.com/users/dashersw), [contributors](https://api.github.com/repos/openserv-labs/sdk/contributors)

**Track record and pivots:** 2024-01-01 GitHub org created [GitHub](https://api.github.com/orgs/openserv-labs); 2024-10-21 first Medium post on AI agents, 2025-01-28 "Shadow Agents" [Medium RSS](https://medium.com/feed/@openservai); 2024-11-07 SERV launched on Ethereum [Blockscout](https://eth.blockscout.com/api/v2/addresses/0x40e3d1A4B2C47d9AA61261F5606136ef73E28042); 2024-12-23 TypeScript SDK repo created (139 stars, last push 2026-02-12) [GitHub](https://api.github.com/orgs/openserv-labs/repos); 2025-03-24 and 03-28 posts position $SERV as the marketplace token and describe the "dash.fun" DeFAI app and fractional ownership of AI agents; 2025-06-24 and 06-27 pivot to a "Telegram-native economy" for agentic apps with an accelerator pitched as the "Y Combinator for Crypto x AI" (revenue matching up to USD 3k per team) [Medium RSS](https://medium.com/feed/@openservai); 2026-02-03 to 02-16 client, skills and openclaw-voice-avatar repos created [GitHub](https://api.github.com/orgs/openserv-labs/repos); 2026-06-26 docs add Build / Launch / Run, an OpenClaw gateway, skills, x402 marketplace agents and ERC-8004; 2026-09-04 (docs) and 09-10 (blog) SERV Reasoning goes public, with a hackathon 2026-09-14 to 09-28 and USD 5,000 in prizes. [Changelog](https://docs.openserv.ai/changelog/overview.md), [hackathon](https://www.openserv.ai/hackathon)

**Trend-hopping score (1-5):** 3. The narrative changed about every 6 to 9 months: agent SDK, then Telegram agentic apps and an accelerator, then DeFAI and a launchpad, then OpenClaw, ERC-8004 and x402, then an enterprise reasoning API. Each phase left a real artifact (SDK, platform, launch API, a public API with docs and a changelog). x402 arrived in the second-to-last phase and is already a footnote in the last. [Medium RSS](https://medium.com/feed/@openservai), [changelog](https://docs.openserv.ai/changelog/overview.md)

**Funding:** Not found. No funding or investor is named on the homepage, team, partners, labs, media kit, roadmap or docs pages; the homepage shows a "Trusted by:" heading with no names. [Homepage](https://openserv.ai), [partners](https://openserv.ai/partners)

**Product and traction:** Reasoning API, OpenAI/Anthropic SDK compatible ("up to 100x cheaper" is a company claim citing a ThoughtProof benchmark). [Product page](https://www.openserv.ai/product) The BRAID paper is arXiv 2512.15959 (the ID suggests December 2025, my inference). [Labs](https://www.openserv.ai/labs) The 2026-09-11 roadmap claims private-beta companies in banking, DeFi, prediction markets and others and names no customers; the partners page has testimonials from five small founders (Kiosa, Roba Labs, Akretic, Tabr, Neol). [Roadmap](https://openserv.ai/blog/roadmap-2026---2027), [Partners](https://openserv.ai/partners) Token holders: 6,728 on Ethereum, 4,286 on Base. [Blockscout](https://eth.blockscout.com/api/v2/addresses/0x40e3d1A4B2C47d9AA61261F5606136ef73E28042), [GeckoTerminal](https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/networks/base/tokens/0x5576d6ed9181f2225aff5282ac0ed29f755437ea/info) GitHub stars: SDK 139, skills 28, mcp-proxy 16. [GitHub](https://api.github.com/orgs/openserv-labs/repos)

**Last six months (2026-04-11 to 2026-10-11):** shipped (dated): 05-29 Playground, OpenAI- and Anthropic-compatible API, BRAID and a public roadmap; 06-12 model catalog expanded; 06-26 Build / Launch / Run, OpenClaw, skills; 07-03 SERV Tools (prompt guard, shadow agent); 08-07 treasury wallets published; 09-18 the token page names OpenServ Inc. as multisig signer; 10-02 staking redesign. [Changelog](https://docs.openserv.ai/changelog/overview.md) Commits: client 2 and mcp-proxy 13, both in 2026-09; none in April to August; the SDK was last pushed 2026-02-12 and skills 2026-04-06, both before the window. [GitHub commits](https://api.github.com/repos/openserv-labs/mcp-proxy/commits), [org repos](https://api.github.com/orgs/openserv-labs/repos)

**Direction:** A reasoning-and-audit layer for regulated sectors; compliance work (SOC 2, ISO 27001, HIPAA, GDPR) is "in progress", fintech pilots and bank proofs-of-concept are the stated targets, SERV is positioned as the accrual asset, and builder, launchpad and x402 are secondary. [Roadmap](https://openserv.ai/blog/roadmap-2026---2027)

**Competitors:** Opinion, not sourced: LLM gateways and verification or guardrail vendors; agent platforms with on-chain payments (Virtuals ACP, Olas, Daydreams, Questflow); launchpads (Clanker, Virtuals).

**Green and red flags:** Green: named team with public professional profiles; dated changelog and roadmap; treasury wallets and multisig entity published; open-source SDK, skills and proxy. [Token docs](https://docs.openserv.ai/what-is-serv/the-serv-token.md) Red: the Base token is a wrapped copy although CoinGecko tags SERV "Base Native" [Base tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x5a57f4432cb8c2bf6e51cb30b7b28f7d1bcaa1ece92a3e28346ba45196748908); no disclosed funding or named customers [Homepage](https://openserv.ai); token roles are still roadmap items; the roadmap blog calls the public API both live and future [Roadmap](https://openserv.ai/blog/roadmap-2026---2027); public code was quiet April to August 2026 [GitHub](https://api.github.com/repos/openserv-labs/mcp-proxy/commits).

**Verdict:** Credible but small; momentum Steady. Opinion: a real, named team with a long public code and docs trail, and the Reasoning API is a real product. The evidence for customers and for x402 use is thin, and the token's stated roles are mostly still promises.

**Not confirmed:** funding; the contents of the x402 Marketplace page; whether dash.fun still exists; the "two years building on Base" claim (a 2026-09-29 X post shown on the homepage; the earliest Base on-chain artifact found is dated 2025-05-19).

#### Profile: HeyElsa ($ELSA)
**One-line:** AI "crypto copilot" that turns plain-language requests into on-chain actions, with a consumer app, a widget/SDK and an agent layer; it also sells pay-per-call DeFi APIs over x402. Side products: Infero (open-weight model gateway), Fixy (in-app copilot) and Aegis (x402 gateway). [Homepage](https://heyelsa.ai), [litepaper](https://blog.heyelsa.ai/heyelsa-the-crypto-agent-layer-litepaper/)

**x402 role:** A first-class API line.
- The x402 site prices about 29 endpoints from USD 0.001 to 0.05 per call (`search_token` USD 0.001, `execute_swap` USD 0.02, `open_perp_position` USD 0.05), paid in USDC or ELSA on Base through the Coinbase CDP facilitator. [x402.heyelsa.ai](https://x402.heyelsa.ai)
- Live check on 2026-10-11: a POST to `x402-v2-api.heyelsa.ai/api/elsa/search_token` returned HTTP 402 with an x402 v2 challenge naming the ELSA contract 0x29cc30f9... as the asset and payTo 0xEcB6175395806A3781AE7E5bae6d0abcB214Ee4D; the USDC route asked for 1,000 raw units (USD 0.001). [Live endpoint](https://x402-v2-api.heyelsa.ai/api/elsa/search_token)
- The ELSA token bytecode contains the EIP-3009 `transferWithAuthorization` and `receiveWithAuthorization` selectors plus `permit`, so it was built to be x402-payable. [Base RPC](https://mainnet.base.org)
- Sequence: `elsa-x402-examples` repo created 2025-12-02; x402scan first indexed `x402-api.heyelsa.ai` on 2026-01-02; token trading started 2026-01-20; the 2026-01-18 litepaper never mentions x402. [GitHub](https://api.github.com/orgs/HeyElsa/repos), [litepaper](https://blog.heyelsa.ai/heyelsa-the-crypto-agent-layer-litepaper/) Fit: a natural extension of the B2B "execution API" line, built before the token and timed with the x402 wave.

**Chains:** Base (ELSA, x402); Mantle (same ELSA address, pool created 2026-01-20); execution across "15+ blockchains" is a company claim. [CoinGecko listing](https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/elsa), [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0x29cc30f9d113b356ce408667aa6433589cecbdca), [x402 site](https://x402.heyelsa.ai)

**Token:** ELSA; Base 0x29cc30f9d113b356ce408667aa6433589cecbdca (also Mantle).
- Contract created 2026-01-07 22:35:05 UTC by direct deploy from 0x82cb33ad1d683570e784d2f7fa17cbedd2329978; not Clanker, Virtuals or Zora. [Base tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x48943806c09df428574c1c4f80611e1d0056ce1b5d2f738e9e24b1f409fec117)
- Launch: self-deployed ERC-20 under an EU MiCA white paper; the offeror is Elsa AI Ltd (BVI); the paper was published 2025-12-15 and notified 2025-12-19, and it gives 2026-01-20 as the start of admission to trading; a PancakeSwap v3 ELSA/USDC pool was created that day. [MiCA white paper](https://docs.heyelsa.ai/elsa-white-paper/heyelsa-mica-whitepaper.md), [GeckoTerminal pool](https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/networks/base/pools/0x83f3aca29bc79b048b99c56013475570acc97ccf)
- Supply and allocation: 1,000,000,000 total; team 7%; company/foundation 34.49%; community 40% (including the initial airdrop); pre-seed 1.4%; seed 9.11%; liquidity 8%. [MiCA white paper](https://docs.heyelsa.ai/elsa-white-paper/heyelsa-mica-whitepaper.md)
- Utility: fee payment and discounts, premium agents, gas abstraction, and payment for inference and SDK use. [Litepaper](https://blog.heyelsa.ai/heyelsa-the-crypto-agent-layer-litepaper/) Real work: only x402 acceptance is verified live; usage is unverified; the litepaper listed the TGE for Q3-Q4 2025 but the contract appeared 2026-01-07.
- Liveness: still trading, PancakeSwap pool liquidity about USD 0.38M. [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0x29cc30f9d113b356ce408667aa6433589cecbdca)
- Contract linkage: HeyElsa's own pages never print the contract address (homepage, x402 site, litepaper, white paper and CoinGecko-application post were checked); the link rests on the live x402 challenge above plus CoinGecko/GeckoTerminal metadata. DexScreener's token profile for ELSA lists moltnbook.com and an unrelated X status link, not heyelsa.ai. [x402 site HTML](https://x402.heyelsa.ai/), [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0x29cc30f9d113b356ce408667aa6433589cecbdca)

**Team:** Dhawal Shah (CEO), Vetrichelvan Jeyapalpandy (CTO) and Rashmi Hegde (COO) are named as the management body in the white paper, so doxxed by name; handles for them were not found from an official source. [MiCA white paper](https://docs.heyelsa.ai/elsa-white-paper/heyelsa-mica-whitepaper.md) Dhawal's blog author page lists posts only. [Author page](https://blog.heyelsa.ai/author/dhawal/) Project X is @HeyElsaAI, linked from the blog. [Blog post](https://blog.heyelsa.ai/heyelsa-raises-3m-to-build-ai-stack-for-crypto/) GitHub org HeyElsa has no public members; the main aether-forge contributor is `vetri02` (display name "Vetri", no X declared), which could not be confirmed as the CTO. [GitHub](https://api.github.com/repos/HeyElsa/aether-forge/contributors)

**Track record and pivots:** 2024-03-25 GitHub org created [GitHub](https://api.github.com/orgs/HeyElsa); 2024-06-20 first blog post "ElsaAI - The Crypto Co-Pilot", 2024-09-16 "HeyElsa is now live"; 2025-01-27 "DeFAI Unleashed"; 2025-04-03 MPC wallets; 2025-06-05 USD 3M raise [Blog index](https://blog.heyelsa.ai); 2025-10-14 widget test dapp; 2025-12-02 x402 examples [GitHub](https://api.github.com/orgs/HeyElsa/repos); 2026-01-16 CoinGecko preview application; 2026-01-18 litepaper [Blog](https://blog.heyelsa.ai/heyelsa-is-applying-for-a-coingecko-preview-listing-for-elsa/); 2026-02-02 `elsa-openclaw` skill pack ("Pay-per-request with x402 micropayments"); 2026-03-27 `elsa-x402-skills`; 2026-04-16 `aether-forge` agent-builder framework (v0.1.0, early-stage). [GitHub](https://github.com/HeyElsa/elsa-openclaw), [aether-forge](https://github.com/HeyElsa/aether-forge)

**Trend-hopping score (1-5):** 3. The core product (a crypto copilot) has stayed the same for two years, but narrative layers were added on a short cycle: DeFAI (2025-01), x402 and ERC-8004 (2025-12), OpenClaw (2026-02), agent builder (2026-04), plus Infero, Fixy and Aegis. [Blog index](https://blog.heyelsa.ai), [GitHub](https://api.github.com/orgs/HeyElsa/repos), [homepage](https://heyelsa.ai)

**Funding:** USD 3M combined pre-seed and seed, announced 2025-06-05; M31 Capital led; others named: Coinbase Ventures' Base Ecosystem Fund, MH Ventures, Absoluta Cap, 2Shares, Levitate Labs and angels from Anoma, ZeroLend, LayerZero, DefiLlama and P2P. [Blog](https://blog.heyelsa.ai/heyelsa-raises-3m-to-build-ai-stack-for-crypto/) The homepage "Backed by" row includes a Base Ecosystem Fund logo. [Homepage](https://heyelsa.ai)

**Product and traction:** Company claims, undated, on the current site: 945K+ wallets, 18.9M+ prompts, USD 503M+ volume. [Homepage](https://heyelsa.ai) Earlier claims: "over $20 million onchain volume" (2025-06-05); the litepaper (2026-01-18) says 300K+ MAUs and 700K+ signups "through Q2 2025", "~$100M" total volume and 30M+ prompts (undated), which is more prompts than today's site, so the claims are inconsistent. [Blog](https://blog.heyelsa.ai/heyelsa-raises-3m-to-build-ai-stack-for-crypto/), [litepaper](https://blog.heyelsa.ai/heyelsa-the-crypto-agent-layer-litepaper/) x402scan footprint, per the sibling note and the x402scan data behind it: 5,189 transactions and USD 47.88 all-time, 2,088 buyers, last settlement 2026-02-02, nothing in the last 30 days; facilitators were Coinbase and PayAI. [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md), [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com) Contradictions with the sibling note: it labels the origin `x402.heyelsa.ai` but x402scan's record is `x402-api.heyelsa.ai` (indexed 2026-01-02), and it says "dead on Base" while on 2026-10-11 the v2 API still issued valid challenges to the same payTo and that v2 host is absent from the x402scan data; what is supported is "no indexed settlement for about 8 months", not "dead". [Live endpoint](https://x402-v2-api.heyelsa.ai/api/elsa/search_token) The x402 page says "Airdrop (Season 1 ended)". [x402 site](https://x402.heyelsa.ai)

**Last six months (2026-04-11 to 2026-10-11):** commits (lead's pull): aether-forge 20 in 2026-04 and 10 in 2026-05; elsa-widget-testdapp 5 in 2026-08; other public repos were last pushed before the window (2026-02-06 to 2026-03-27). [GitHub](https://api.github.com/orgs/HeyElsa/repos) Infero, Fixy, Aegis and CLI pages exist but none is dated; Infero says price tracking "started Aug 21", with no year. [Infero](https://www.infero.xyz/), [Fixy](https://heyelsa.ai/fixy), [Aegis](https://www.aegisx402.io/)

**Direction:** Developer and agent infrastructure (x402 API, OpenClaw skills, agent builder, model gateway) alongside the consumer copilot, with an EU MiCA route for the token. [Aether-forge](https://github.com/HeyElsa/aether-forge), [MiCA white paper](https://docs.heyelsa.ai/elsa-white-paper/heyelsa-mica-whitepaper.md)

**Competitors:** Opinion, not sourced: other DeFi copilots and agent-execution APIs (Bankr, Otto, Daydreams-style routers); x402 DeFi data sellers such as Nansen and Messari.

**Green and red flags:** Green: named founders and investors including the Base Ecosystem Fund; a MiCA filing, unusual for this group; an x402 v2 endpoint that is live and uses a token built for it; MIT-licensed code. [Blog](https://blog.heyelsa.ai/heyelsa-raises-3m-to-build-ai-stack-for-crypto/), [MiCA](https://docs.heyelsa.ai/elsa-white-paper/heyelsa-mica-whitepaper.md) Red: x402 volume is USD 48 lifetime [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md); engagement claims undated and inconsistent; the TGE slipped about one quarter; the white paper has internal gaps (a retained figure of 37.490% against a 34.49% allocation) [MiCA](https://docs.heyelsa.ai/elsa-white-paper/heyelsa-mica-whitepaper.md); the contract address is not on HeyElsa's own pages; the DexScreener profile points elsewhere; the careers page lists only two marketing/comms roles [Careers](https://heyelsa.ai/careers).

**Verdict:** Credible but small; momentum Steady. Opinion: HeyElsa has real shipping and unusually formal paperwork, and its x402 work is genuine engineering that predates its token. Lifetime x402 revenue is tiny and the usage figures need independent proof.

**Not confirmed:** whether ELSA was listed on Coinbase or Kraken (the white paper only says "proposed"); who set the DexScreener profile; usage claims; whether Aegis is a HeyElsa product (the Aegis page names no builder, but the HeyElsa nav links it); the v2 settlement history.

**Tracking card:** site https://heyelsa.ai · docs https://docs.heyelsa.ai · x402 https://x402.heyelsa.ai · GitHub https://github.com/HeyElsa · X @HeyElsaAI · people handles not found · Telegram and Farcaster not found · Base and Mantle 0x29cc30f9d113b356ce408667aa6433589cecbdca.

**Sources (accessed 2026-10-11):** heyelsa.ai (home, /fixy, /careers); x402.heyelsa.ai; the live x402 endpoint; blog.heyelsa.ai (raise, CoinGecko application, litepaper, author page, index); docs.heyelsa.ai MiCA white paper; infero.xyz; aegisx402.io; GitHub API and READMEs; DexScreener; GeckoTerminal; Base RPC; the sibling note; x402scan.

#### Profile: Neurobro ($BRO)
**One-line:** Consumer AI-finance app with research chat, news feed, whale tracking, portfolio and the non-custodial NeuroWallet, built on 200+ specialised agents; it also sells a subscription API and a pay-per-query x402 endpoint. The operator is NeuroFoundation, LLC (Delaware) and the GitHub org is Axioma AI Labs. [Site](https://neurobro.ai), [company docs](https://docs.neurobro.ai/foundation/company.md)

**x402 role:** A small, separate side product. `x402.neurobro.ai`, `POST /api/v1/query`, costs USD 1.00 USDC on Base and exposes 40+ tools with no account or key. [x402 docs](https://docs.neurobro.ai/technical/other-products/x402.md) NeuroAPI (from USD 49.99 per month) is the main developer product and the docs say x402 access is "governed separately". [API page](https://neurobro.ai/api) Live check on 2026-10-11: the endpoint returns an x402 **v1** challenge (`maxAmountRequired` 1,000,000, USDC, payTo 0x090d5C06f8B33A1F1bD1816C44a87030d83bf7a9). [Live endpoint](https://x402.neurobro.ai/api/v1/query) Timing: the `x402-neurobro` repo was created 2025-10-28; x402scan first indexed `api.neurobro.ai` on 2025-10-27 and `x402.neurobro.ai` on 2025-10-28, about five days after the PING mint. [GitHub](https://api.github.com/orgs/axioma-ai-labs/repos), [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com) The main site and the 20-entry monthly changelog never mention x402 or the API. [Changelog](https://docs.neurobro.ai/products/neurobro/changelog.md) Judgement: a bolt-on, lightly maintained.

**Chains:** Base and Solana for NeuroWallet trading; x402 and the token on Base. [Site](https://neurobro.ai)

**Token:** BRO (on-chain name "Neurobro by Virtuals"); Base 0xc796e499cc8f599a2a8280825d8bda92f7a895e0; supply about 993.0M on Base. [Base RPC](https://mainnet.base.org)
- Launch: Virtuals Protocol bonding-curve launch. The Virtuals agent record was created 2024-12-07 16:18 UTC and the pre-token 2024-12-07 16:19:45 UTC through the Virtuals bonding contract; the final token was created 2024-12-08 13:44:49 UTC when it graduated to a Uniswap v2 BRO/VIRTUAL pool; the record shows factory "BONDING" and category "IP MIRROR". [Virtuals API](https://api.virtuals.io/api/virtuals?filters%5BtokenAddress%5D%5B%24eqi%5D=0xc796e499cc8f599a2a8280825d8bda92f7a895e0), [pre-token tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x8b5d1b8c7fbb8315259c663a024fd483b2fd8ea7949fb243f1871ba359128d09), [graduation tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x925f02d9173acf50c7681023084711b67ae03d5502606a3bfd8d2b346588c2b0)
- Stated utility: only on the Virtuals listing ("unlock premium access to neurodex.ai", the old product name). [Virtuals API](https://api.virtuals.io/api/virtuals?filters%5BtokenAddress%5D%5B%24eqi%5D=0xc796e499cc8f599a2a8280825d8bda92f7a895e0) The official plans, company and vision pages never mention BRO, pricing is card or app-store, and the Terms of Use (updated 2026-10-05) mention `$BRO` once, in the acknowledgement, defining no use. [Plans](https://docs.neurobro.ai/neurobro/plans.md), [Terms](https://docs.neurobro.ai/legal/terms-of-use) Real work in the product: none found.
- Liveness: still trading in the Virtuals v2 pool (about USD 88k in reserves, near-zero daily volume); holders 119,132. [Virtuals API](https://api.virtuals.io/api/virtuals?filters%5BtokenAddress%5D%5B%24eqi%5D=0xc796e499cc8f599a2a8280825d8bda92f7a895e0)

[Changelog](https://docs.neurobro.ai/products/neurobro/changelog.md)

[Site](https://neurobro.ai) Trading fees 0.50% / 0.40% / 0.30% by plan, USD 0.25 minimum. [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md), [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com)

[GitHub commits](https://api.github.com/repos/axioma-ai-labs/neuroapi-cookbook/commits)

**Direction:** Multi-asset (crypto, tokenized stocks, commodities, perps) research and trading in one app, plus API revenue; the token is not part of the documented plan. [Changelog](https://docs.neurobro.ai/products/neurobro/changelog.md)

**Competitors:** Opinion, not sourced: other AI-finance and whale-tracking apps (Nansen, Arkham-style terminals), other Virtuals research agents, Bankr-style trading agents.

**Green and red flags:** Green: named co-founders and an advisor with linked handles; a Delaware entity; a long, dated, regular changelog; app-store apps; a live x402 endpoint. [Live endpoint](https://x402.neurobro.ai/api/v1/query)

**Verdict:** Serious builder (of the consumer app); momentum Steady. Opinion: the clearest shipper in this group. x402 and $BRO are side issues; the token looks like a Virtuals-era artifact the product team does not lean on.

**Not confirmed:** funding; user counts; whether the Virtuals LP is the only meaningful market (DexScreener showed no pairs, GeckoTerminal showed two pools); the link between GitHub `gromdimon` and Dima.

#### Profile: Cod3x ($CDX)
**One-line:** AI perpetual-futures trading terminal: users describe a strategy in plain English ("MetaChat") and agents trade on Hyperliquid (live); Kraken, Binance, Kalshi and Polymarket are listed as queued. It runs on credits and subscriptions, with CDX staking discounts. [Site](https://cod3x.org)

**x402 role:** A payment rail inside the product. Changelog: Oct 2025 "The x402 payment protocol — facilitator service, routes, and schema"; Dec 2025 "Pay-per-use (x402) gasless USDC payments"; Jun 2026 "x402 pay-as-you-go top-ups". [Changelog](https://cod3x.org/docs/reference/changelog) Docs: an x402 option pays in USDC on Base with gasless EIP-3009 signatures, and the terminal's "Buy" button opens a technical report paid via x402. [Docs](https://docs.cod3x.org/llms-full.txt) The homepage does not mention x402, and no x402scan seller footprint matched by domain, so on-chain volume is unconfirmed. [Site](https://cod3x.org), [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md) Judgement: a natural fit for pay-as-you-go billing, added in the Oct 2025 wave.

**Chains:** Base (CDX, x402); a Solana CDX exists per CoinGecko; trading venues are Hyperliquid, GMX and Lighter. [CoinGecko listing](https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/cod3x), [docs](https://docs.cod3x.org/llms.txt)

**Token:** CDX; Base 0xc0d3700000c0e32716863323bfd936b54a1633d1 (vanity address).
- Created 2025-02-11 17:28:11 UTC through the CreateX deployer 0xba5ed099633d3b313e4d5f7bdc1305d3c28ba5ed from EOA 0x3d0c000adf317206fa4a3201a8f8c926ef394fad. [Base tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0xd1c6b69b1360debd6d7d570fae579324ffd485626da33094a092d3dc3edccb60)
- Launch: a self-deployed vanity ERC-20, not Clanker or Virtuals; the first pool, a Uniswap v4 CDX/ETH pool, dates from 2025-02-18; allocation and vesting not found (the emission-schedule pages are empty). [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0xc0d3700000c0e32716863323bfd936b54a1633d1), [docs](https://docs.cod3x.org/cdx-token/emission-schedule.md)
- Supply: docs say max 100,000,000 and about 50,000,000 circulating; Base totalSupply is 47.97M; the gap is unexplained (burns or the Solana side). [Docs](https://docs.cod3x.org/cdx-token/what-is-cdx.md), [Base RPC](https://mainnet.base.org)
- Utility: staking for credit discounts of 10 to 30%, USDC yield from profit, and buy-and-burn; staking went live in the Feb 2026 changelog. - Liveness: still trading but thin, Uniswap v4 liquidity about USD 29k and daily volume near zero. [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0xc0d3700000c0e32716863323bfd936b54a1633d1)

**Team:** Pseudonymous. The site's team component lists nicknames with no handles and the GitHub org has no public members: Bebis (CEO, "founded COD3X in 2022"), Ewhal4 (AI Developer), Munchies (ML Engineer), Steven (Product), SAMSoN (COD3X Foundation), Wetzo (DeFi Engineer). **Track record and pivots:** The changelog starts May 2023 as a "Moon" vault library, then AI wallet infrastructure (2024); in 2024-04 it teased "100,000 AI agents". [Changelog](https://cod3x.org/docs/reference/changelog) Repo descriptions point to an earlier DeFi lineage (Reaper vault code "since 2021" and a CDP lending platform on Optimism, Mode, Mantle and Scroll); the CDP and vault repos were archived in 2024. [GitHub](https://github.com/Cod3x-Labs) 2024-12 first trading bot; 2025-01 agents and plugins; 2025-02-11 CDX contract; 2025-09 pay-as-you-go billing; 2025-10 x402 and "EIP-8004 trustless agents"; 2025-12 terminal rebuild; 2026-06 v2.0 closed beta (MetaChat); 2026-07 v2.0 public beta. [Changelog](https://cod3x.org/docs/reference/changelog)

**Trend-hopping score (1-5):** 3. Four product narratives in about 30 months: DeFi vaults and lending, AI wallet infrastructure, no-code agent creation (the CoinGecko tag "AI Agent Launchpad"), and an AI perps terminal. A detailed changelog shows real shipping at each stage. [Changelog](https://cod3x.org/docs/reference/changelog), [CoinGecko listing](https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/cod3x)

[Site](https://cod3x.org) Holders on Base: 59,221 (holders, not users). [GeckoTerminal](https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/networks/base/tokens/0xc0d3700000c0e32716863323bfd936b54a1633d1/info) x402scan: none matched. [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md)

[Changelog](https://cod3x.org/docs/reference/changelog) Public GitHub: no pushes after 2026-03-03 (Reliquary), so zero commits in the window. [GitHub](https://api.github.com/orgs/Cod3x-Labs/repos)

**Direction:** A credit-billed AI trading terminal with venue expansion, a creator and plugin marketplace, and revenue sharing to stakers. [Docs](https://docs.cod3x.org/llms.txt)

**Competitors:** Opinion, not sourced: Hyperliquid-native bots and AI trading terminals, no-code strategy builders, and other Virtuals or Bankr trading agents.

[Changelog](https://cod3x.org/docs/reference/changelog) Red: pseudonymous team; no funding information; dormant public code; an unexplained supply gap and tier inconsistency; the "400,000 agents" claim is unsupported; very thin token liquidity.

#### Profile: Capminal ($CAP)
**One-line:** An AI terminal and DeFAI agent ("Captain Dackie") built by 404AI Labs, with a token-launch UI (Orbs, with Liquid, Clanker and Virtuals launchers), an OpenAI-compatible inference gateway (OpenCAP) and "Perpetual AI Compute", where staking CAP earns daily inference credit. [Site](https://www.capminal.ai)

**x402 role:** Both seller and client. Seller: `GET www.capminal.ai/api/x402/research`, "AI-powered Token Deep Research API", listed in the Bazaar at USD 0.01 per call on Base USDC, 15 calls from 7 payers in the last 30 days, last called 2026-10-11. [CDP Bazaar discovery API](https://api.cdp.coinbase.com/platform/v2/x402/discovery/resources) Docs: Coinbase CDP facilitator, settlement on Base in USDC, upgraded to x402 v2 on 2025-12-17; two further APIs are "T.B.U". [x402 docs](https://capminal.gitbook.io/docs/capminal/x402.md) Client: an "x402 Orchestrator" lets the agent discover and pay any x402 API from the terminal. [Docs](https://capminal.gitbook.io/docs/llms-full.txt) x402scan first indexed `www.capminal.ai` on 2025-10-27, five days after the PING mint. [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com) The homepage never mentions x402, and the docs index lists the research API as "(paused)" while traffic continues, so the docs and the live listing disagree. [Site](https://www.capminal.ai), [docs index](https://capminal.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt) Judgement: a maintained bolt-on with negligible use.

**Chains:** Base only (CAP, contracts, x402). [Contracts README](https://github.com/capminal/capminal-contracts)

**Token:** CAP (on-chain name "Capminal by Virtuals"); Base 0xbfa733702305280f066d470afdfa784fa70e2649.
- Created 2025-05-23 16:01:03 UTC through the Virtuals bonding contract 0x42f4f5a3389ca0bed694de339f4d432acddb1ea9; the sender, 0x81f7ca6af86d..., also created other Virtuals tokens, so it is a Virtuals-side wallet. [Base tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x17b95f22404fd95e152a499d2564a2d7759ed7c74056882277282b80f8e7f54b)
- Launch: a Virtuals Genesis (id 105) with a 24-hour sale window from 2025-05-22 16:00 to 2025-05-23 16:00 UTC, then launched 2025-05-23 16:01:12 UTC. [Virtuals API](https://api.virtuals.io/api/virtuals?filters%5BtokenAddress%5D%5B%24eqi%5D=0xbfa733702305280f066d470afdfa784fa70e2649)
- Allocation: max supply 1,000,000,000; public sale 37.5%; liquidity 12.5%; Virtuals stakers and yappers 3%; CAP Points 3%; DACKIE Premium 3%; marketing 1%; "Burned" 40%. The categories sum to 100%, yet the vesting text refers to "Team and DAO Treasury" and "Early backers" allocations that do not appear, so the table is incomplete; a monthly buy-and-burn funded by trading and Clanker-UI fees is stated. [Tokenomics](https://capminal.gitbook.io/docs/cap-token/tokenomics.md)
- Utility: stake CAP, lock the receipt to mint CAPU, and each CAPU gives USD 1 of OpenCAP inference credit per day; the contracts are open source (MIT). [Docs](https://capminal.gitbook.io/docs/llms-full.txt), [contracts README](https://github.com/capminal/capminal-contracts) Real work: documented; on-chain use not verified (the metrics page loads empty). [Metrics](https://www.capminal.ai/metrics)
- Liveness: still trading, CAP/VIRTUAL pool about USD 97k with tiny volume; holders 14,041. [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0xbfa733702305280f066d470afdfa784fa70e2649)

**Team:** Pseudonymous, listed on the 404AI Labs team page (linked from the Capminal footer): AndreaPN (Founder, x.com/AndreaPN), Key (Core Dev, no link), Dustin (Partnerships Lead, x.com/dustinverse), Erious (Product Manager, x.com/Erious0520), Dackie (KOL, x.com/ItsDackie), Jimndes (Designer, x.com/Jimndes). [Team page](https://404ai.io/team) AndreaPN is verified three ways: the team page, the Virtuals creator account (verified X and GitHub), and GitHub `andreapn` as the only contributor to `capminal-contracts` and author of the agent skills (confidence high). [Virtuals API](https://api.virtuals.io/api/virtuals?filters%5BtokenAddress%5D%5B%24eqi%5D=0xbfa733702305280f066d470afdfa784fa70e2649), [contributors](https://api.github.com/repos/capminal/capminal-contracts/contributors) 404AI Labs lists DackieSwap among its products. #### Observations across these profiles (OpenServ, HeyElsa, Neurobro, Cod3x, Capminal, Hive Intelligence, FLOCK)
1. x402 timing cluster: Neurobro (x402scan first indexed 2025-10-27/28, repo created 2025-10-28) and Capminal (2025-10-27) went live about five days after the PING mint; Cod3x logs its x402 facilitator work in Oct 2025; HeyElsa followed on 2025-12-02; for OpenServ the earliest x402 date found is 2026-06-26. [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com), [Cod3x changelog](https://cod3x.org/docs/reference/changelog), [HeyElsa GitHub](https://api.github.com/orgs/HeyElsa/repos), [OpenServ changelog](https://docs.openserv.ai/changelog/overview.md)
2. x402 revenue is near zero everywhere it is measurable: USD 47.88 (Elsa), 42.14 (Neurobro) and 11.38 (Capminal) lifetime; no footprint matched for OpenServ, Cod3x, Hive or FLOCK. [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md)
3. Same standards stack in the same window: ERC-8004 appears in OpenServ's docs, Cod3x's Oct 2025 changelog, HeyElsa's site and 404AI Labs' site (which also names ERC-8183). [OpenServ](https://docs.openserv.ai/vibecode/openclaw/erc8004-identity.md), [Cod3x](https://cod3x.org/docs/reference/changelog), [HeyElsa](https://heyelsa.ai), [404AI](https://404ai.io/)
4. "Skills" and OpenClaw packaging in 2026: Capminal `agent-skills` (2026-02-03), OpenServ `skills` (2026-02-05) and `openclaw-voice-avatar` (2026-02-16), HeyElsa `elsa-openclaw` (2026-02-02) and `elsa-x402-skills` (2026-03-27), Neurobro `neurobro-skills` (2026-05-17), Hive `hive-skills` (2026-05-30): six repos across five of the seven projects. [OpenServ](https://api.github.com/orgs/openserv-labs/repos), [HeyElsa](https://api.github.com/orgs/HeyElsa/repos), [Capminal](https://api.github.com/orgs/capminal/repos), [Neurobro](https://api.github.com/orgs/axioma-ai-labs/repos), [Hive](https://api.github.com/orgs/hive-intel/repos)
5. Virtuals re-use: Neurobro (2024-12) and Capminal (2025-05) launched through Virtuals, then returned to its agent-commerce layer (Neurobro as an ACP seller; Capminal's Degen Claw skills, 2026-04-09). [Virtuals API](https://api.virtuals.io/api/virtuals?filters%5BtokenAddress%5D%5B%24eqi%5D=0xc796e499cc8f599a2a8280825d8bda92f7a895e0), [ACP docs](https://docs.neurobro.ai/technical/other-products/acp.md), [Capminal GitHub](https://api.github.com/orgs/capminal/repos)
6. Token-launch features for third parties: OpenServ Launch (docs 2026-06-26), Capminal Orbs/Clanker UI, FLOCK FOMO, Cod3x's agent marketplace. [OpenServ](https://docs.openserv.ai/changelog/overview.md), [Capminal](https://www.capminal.ai), [FLOCK](https://www.flock.io/blog/flockio-investor-report-q2-2026), [Cod3x](https://docs.cod3x.org/llms.txt)
7. Hot-topic adoption in mid-2026: Hyperliquid perps (Cod3x 2026-05, Neurobro 2026-08), tokenized stocks (Neurobro 2026-07, FLOCK pools 2026-08/09). [Cod3x](https://cod3x.org/docs/reference/changelog), [Neurobro](https://docs.neurobro.ai/products/neurobro/changelog.md), [FLOCK](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0x5ab3d4c385b400f3abb49e80de2faf6a88a7b691)
8. Token integration is the weak link: the tokens for Neurobro and Hive have no role in current official materials; SERV's payment and staking roles are still roadmap; only ELSA (x402 payment asset, EIP-3009), CDX (staking discounts) and FLOCK (training incentives) show documented, live roles. [Neurobro docs](https://docs.neurobro.ai/neurobro/plans.md), [Hive docs](https://docs.hiveintelligence.xyz/llms-full.txt), [OpenServ](https://openserv.ai)
- Could not confirm (WebSearch unavailable) and access limits, per the sub-agent: funding for OpenServ, Neurobro, Cod3x, Capminal, Hive and FLOCK; X content (x.com returned HTTP 402 to WebFetch); Farcaster beyond Capminal's capminal.eth; independent usage and user numbers (HeyElsa 945K wallets and USD 503M, Neurobro 390K users, Cod3x 400,000 agents); pages it could not read (platform.openserv.ai/x402-services, launch.openserv.ai/launchpad, dash.fun, moltnbook.com, usemoon.ai, the Benzinga release, base.blockscout.com); GitBook "Agent Instructions" text on docs.cod3x.org and docs.flock.io asking agents to call a docs query endpoint was not followed; the Base SERV "wrapped" reading rests on a selector match plus bytecode strings.

#### Profile: Aubrai (AUBRAI, "Aubrai by Bio")
**One-line:** Longevity research agent built by VitaDAO and Bio Protocol with Aubrey de Grey's LEV Foundation. It answers questions, feeds the RMR2 mouse-lifespan study, and charges per request over x402. [Bio launch post, 2025-08-25](https://bio.xyz/blog/decentralized-science-project-aubrai-launches-on-base-to-tackle-science-funding-s-valley-of-death)

**x402 role:** Live. Unpaid requests on 2026-10-11 (about 11:25 UTC) to x402-api.aubr.ai returned HTTP 402, x402 v2, with USDC on Base as the only asset. Prices: chat 0.02 USD, literature search 0.03 USD, deep-research job 1.00 USD. [chat](https://x402-api.aubr.ai/api/chat), [literature](https://x402-api.aubr.ai/api/literature), [deep research](https://x402-api.aubr.ai/api/deep-research/start) The CDP Bazaar list also holds two encrypted variants (prompt sealed, processed in an attested enclave). [CDP Bazaar discovery API](https://api.cdp.coinbase.com/platform/v2/x402/discovery/resources) The pay-to address 0x44dC2dceaA22c7EF6B2E2cF2362Fd3FB476b7c2C is a Safe v1.4.1 with 3 of 6 owners (eth_call check); docs.aubr.ai lists the same address as the AUBRAI treasury. [Safe](https://basescan.org/address/0x44dC2dceaA22c7EF6B2E2cF2362Fd3FB476b7c2C), [docs links](https://docs.aubr.ai/resources/markdown.md) Bio said on 2025-10-29 it would add x402 to BioAgents and on 2025-11-11 that Aubrai had taken its first x402 payment. [Bio 2025-10-29](https://bio.xyz/blog/bio-weekly-season-2-preview-x402-payment-rails-for-desci-agents-aerodrome-pool-launcher), [Bio 2025-11-11](https://bio.xyz/blog/x402-meets-bioagents-ai-scientists-rewiring-the-science-economy) The AUBRAI token is not accepted as payment. The fit is natural: a per-question research agent needs no accounts.

**Chains:** Base for the token, the Safes and x402 settlement; VitaDAO says valid hypotheses are hashed on Base through Molecule's Proof-of-Invention. [VitaDAO 2025-09-12](https://www.vitadao.com/blog/meet-aubrai-and-his-100k-hypothesis-challenge-for-longevity-research)

**Token:** AUBRAI; Base 0x9d56c29e820dd13b0580b185d0e0dc301d27581d.
- Contract created 2025-08-27 12:29:55 UTC (block 34754224): a Safe execTransaction sent from EOA 0x417b4adc279743fc49f047c323fc668db9e600d8 to Safe 0xe57a09c3fdfb6bd2b8ebc46e0ff884876fae60fd, which is still the token's owner() (checked on-chain). [Base tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0xcf8dadbfb862064982e0c79552ff249b6743a67987dbadeb75f361e8cfabba94)
- Method: Bio Protocol Launchpad (Bio's 2025-08-25 post says the token launches there that week; no dated launchpad record found). [Bio launch post](https://bio.xyz/blog/decentralized-science-project-aubrai-launches-on-base-to-tackle-science-funding-s-valley-of-death)
- Supply 2,000,000: 20% launchpad sale, 6% liquidity, 15% treasury, 20.1% RMR2 initial funders, 10% LEVF, 22% VitaDAO, 6.9% BIO; parts vest over 4 years. [docs tokenomics](https://docs.aubr.ai/resources/editor/tokenomics.md)
- Stated utility: the docs give none; Bio's launch post says holders vote on funding and share revenue from commercialised discoveries; VitaDAO says trading fees can fund experiments; Bio's 2025-09-22 newsletter claims about 275K USD a month of research funding from market-making fees (self-reported). [Bio 2025-09-22](https://bio.xyz/blog/bio-weekly-6-9m-raise-new-ignition-sales-desci-lending-w-aave-rna-molecules-aubrai-nets-275k) Real work: fee-funded research and governance as claimed; none in x402.
- Still trading; the BIO/AUBRAI pool holds about 144K USD. [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0x9d56c29e820dd13b0580b185d0e0dc301d27581d) CoinGecko relays a GoPlus notice that the creator can alter the contract; the owner is the 3-of-6 Safe. [CoinGecko](https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/aubrai-by-bio)

**Team:** Doxxed institutions and scientist: VitaDAO, Bio Protocol, LEV Foundation; Aubrey de Grey is named as scientific lead and quoted; Paul Kohlhaas is named as Bio Protocol's founder in the same post; no Aubrai engineers are named. [Bio launch post](https://bio.xyz/blog/decentralized-science-project-aubrai-launches-on-base-to-tackle-science-funding-s-valley-of-death) X @Aubrai_ is linked from docs.aubr.ai (high confidence); no personal handles recorded because no official source links any. [docs links](https://docs.aubr.ai/resources/markdown.md) GitHub: none for Aubrai; Bio's org holds the BioAgents framework and a small aubrai-agent template last pushed 2025-02-11. [bio-xyz repos](https://github.com/bio-xyz)

**Track record and pivots:** 2025-08-25 launch announced; 2025-08-27 contract created [Bio](https://bio.xyz/blog/decentralized-science-project-aubrai-launches-on-base-to-tackle-science-funding-s-valley-of-death), [tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0xcf8dadbfb862064982e0c79552ff249b6743a67987dbadeb75f361e8cfabba94); 2025-09-12 VitaDAO runs a 100K USD hypothesis challenge around the agent [VitaDAO](https://www.vitadao.com/blog/meet-aubrai-and-his-100k-hypothesis-challenge-for-longevity-research); 2025-10-21 VitaSTEM IP token launches on Bio's launchpad, with 20% held by the Aubrai treasury [Bio](https://bio.xyz/blog/bio-weekly-launchpad-in-review-ai-driven-stem-cell-research-upbit-listing-ip-pipeline-update); 2025-11-07 x402scan registers the first x402 origin; 2026-01-28 Bio launches BIOS, built on BioAgents, and Aubrai is to become a BIOS sub-agent later [Bio](https://bio.xyz/blog/introducing-bios); 2026-03-13 second x402 origin (x402-api.aubr.ai) appears; 2026-06-15 BioAgents repo archived. [GitHub](https://github.com/bio-xyz/BioAgents)

**Trend-hopping score (1-5):** 2. One focus, longevity research, since 2025-08; x402 came in 2025-11 as a payment layer for the same agent. Bio pushed x402 across its agents in its Season 2 messaging on 2025-10-29, so the timing also follows the wave. [Bio](https://bio.xyz/blog/bio-weekly-season-2-preview-x402-payment-rails-for-desci-agents-aerodrome-pool-launcher)

**Funding:** No venture round for Aubrai found. Money came from the launchpad sale (amount raised not found), the RMR2 funders' allocation and the claimed fee flow. **Product and traction:** x402scan (Base, USDC) shows 26,885 payments and about 764 USD to the treasury address, 2025-11-07 to 2026-10-10, peaking in March and April 2026 (7,669 and 13,251 payments, up to 59 buyers a day); monthly counts since then run 369 to 1,996. [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com/api/trpc/public.stats.bucketed) Cross-check: the sibling note reports 14.5K tx / 321 USD in six months and 2.3K calls in 30 days for x402-api.aubr.ai; the sub-agent re-derived 14,481 tx / 321.44 USD (2026-04-11 to 2026-10-11) and 2,285 tx / 62.66 USD (last 30 days); no contradiction. [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md) VitaDAO's 2026-09-25 newsletter says its Vita App is powered by Aubrai and mentions a private mode and an Aubrai Academy. [VitaDAO](https://www.vitadao.com/blog/vitadao-longevity-newsletter-september-26) aubr.ai returned HTTP 403 to automated requests behind a Vercel security checkpoint, so the consumer app is unassessed. [aubr.ai](https://aubr.ai)

**Last six months (2026-04-11 to 2026-10-11):** x402 payments by month (April is the whole month): Apr 13,251 / 329 USD; May 1,127 / 26; Jun 369 / 18; Jul 511 / 11; Aug 699 / 14; Sep 1,996 / 44; Oct 1-10 1,250 / 37. [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com/api/trpc/public.stats.bucketed) Bazaar entries were updated 2026-09-14, 2026-09-24 and 2026-10-08 to 10-10. bio-xyz/BioAgents commits per month: April 32 (includes April 1-10), May 9, June 1; archived 2026-06-15. [GitHub](https://github.com/bio-xyz/BioAgents) None of the 12 Bio blog titles dated 2026-04-06 to 2026-10-06 mentions Aubrai, and neither of the two posts read in full does. [Bio index p1](https://www.bio.xyz/blog), [p2](https://www.bio.xyz/blog?page=2), [2026-10-06](https://www.bio.xyz/blog/bio-protocol-september-2026-open-science-and-desci-updates)

**Direction:** Paid API plus embedding in VitaDAO's Vita App, with privacy features, and a planned BIOS sub-agent role. [VitaDAO](https://www.vitadao.com/blog/vitadao-longevity-newsletter-september-26), [Bio](https://bio.xyz/blog/introducing-bios) Opinion: the archived framework repo suggests Bio's engineering moved to BIOS, so Aubrai now depends mostly on VitaDAO's interest.

**Competitors:** Bio's repo description names Edison's Kosmos, Sakana AI and K-Dense as inspirations and an org repo wraps the FutureHouse API; BIOS is Bio's in-house sibling. [BioAgents](https://github.com/bio-xyz/BioAgents), [Bio](https://bio.xyz/blog/introducing-bios)

**Green and red flags:** Green: verified live 402s; pay-to is a 3-of-6 Safe that also owns the token; named institutions; allocations and vesting published. [docs](https://docs.aubr.ai/resources/editor/tokenomics.md) Red: lifetime x402 income about 764 USD, most of it in two spring months [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com/api/trpc/public.stats.bucketed); BioAgents archived and Bio's recent posts silent on Aubrai [GitHub](https://github.com/bio-xyz/BioAgents); a GoPlus notice on the contract [CoinGecko](https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/aubrai-by-bio). Not confirmed: launchpad sale size and date; the 275K USD monthly claim; aubr.ai content; who operates the x402 server; the cause of the March-April 2026 spike.

**Verdict:** Credible but small; momentum Slowing. Opinion: backing is real and x402 works as designed, but income is tiny and Bio's framework repo was shut. Watch the Vita App integration.

#### Profile: Ethy AI (ETHY)
**One-line:** Anonymous-team AI trading and automation assistant on Base and OKX X Layer. Users describe strategies in plain language, pay a monthly subscription, and can call a paid x402 API for trading signals. [ethyai.app](https://ethyai.app)

**x402 role:** Live as a secondary revenue path. api.ethyai.app states that its /paid/v1/* routes are x402 and its /v1/* routes use API keys. Unpaid GETs on 2026-10-11 returned 402 (x402 v2, USDC on Base) for base/signal at 0.05 USD, base/indicators at 0.05 USD, base/score at 0.10 USD and base/analysis (LLM chart analysis) at 0.25 USD; the pay-to address is 0xe8067E3C72F18054De14E4950480c093156130f8. [signal](https://api.ethyai.app/paid/v1/base/signal), [analysis](https://api.ethyai.app/paid/v1/base/analysis) The xlayer variants returned "unknown endpoint" for the paths tried. Neither the docs nor the roadmap mention x402. [roadmap](https://ethy-ai-1.gitbook.io/docs/roadmap.md) Fit: plausible (per-call data for agents), but the payments are tiny.

**Chains:** Base and OKX X Layer; Hyperliquid (Arbitrum) is listed as coming soon; x402 settles on Base. [ethyai.app](https://ethyai.app)

**Token:** ETHY; Base 0xc44141a684f6aa4e36cd9264ab55550b03c88643.
- Virtuals agent (bonding-curve route, pre-token 0xA6197D5E...). Contract created 2025-01-17 08:04:43 UTC (block 25155868) by EOA 0x4d3877abc39d131a39ce21dd0675ad3c1e3f3f32 calling factory 0xf66dea7b3e897cd44a5a231c61b6b4423d613259; the transaction also moves VIRTUAL and sets up an ERC-6551 account. [Base tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x6a0064df30cbf76ca002f7907ec9639d683c5b56e4d4987bb151452cee87d433), [Virtuals API](https://api.virtuals.io/api/virtuals?filters%5BtokenAddress%5D%5B%24eq%5D=0xc44141a684f6aa4e36cd9264ab55550b03c88643) Supply 1B; 13,597 holders.

#### Profile: Sally A1C (A1C)
**One-line:** Singapore-registered metabolic-health company (A1C PTE. LTD.) selling a CGM-based app with an AI coach called Sally, a sweetener (0spike) and a metered API for AI agents; the A1C token sits alongside. [a1c.io llms.txt](https://a1c.io/llms.txt)

**x402 role:** None found. Sally Skills (MCP and REST at sally.a1c.io) uses a bearer API key and a prepaid wallet; top-ups are by card (Stripe) or USDC on Base, and the docs say paid calls return an application-level `payment_required` 402 when the balance is too low. [docs repo](https://github.com/a1c-ai-agent/sally-skills), [console llms.txt](https://console.a1c.io/llms.txt) A POST to /mcp on 2026-10-11 listed ten tools, seven priced 0.001 to 0.008 USD per call. [sally.a1c.io/mcp](https://sally.a1c.io/mcp) Per-call micropricing for agents is the shape x402 serves, but it is not adopted: no x402 text in the docs or llms.txt, and the sibling note matched no x402 footprint. CoinGecko still lists the token in its x402 category. [CoinGecko](https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/sally-a1c), [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md)

[one-year post](https://www.facticity.ai/post/one-year-of-facy-from-virtuals-genesis-to-beyond) Opinion: per-claim verification would suit x402, but the stated path uses ACP and MCP.

**Competitors:** Facticity's benchmark names Perplexity, Bing Copilot and Originality.AI. [site](https://www.facticity.ai) From general knowledge: X Community Notes.

Not confirmed: platform user numbers; the Genesis raise amount.

**Verdict:** Credible but small; momentum Steady. Opinion: the most real product in this group, but x402 is not part of it.

Not confirmed: the Sablier vesting; the official launch date; a home-page claim of six years of engineering, which About does not repeat.

**Verdict:** Speculative; momentum Steady (early, little verifiable). Opinion: a real front end and tools, thin original engineering in public.

#### Profile: MiroShark (MIROSHARK)
**One-line:** Open-source (AGPL-3.0) multi-agent simulator that tests how a document, launch or headline lands on a Twitter-like feed, a Reddit-like forum and a prediction market, and sells runs at 1 USD over x402. [miroshark.xyz](https://miroshark.xyz), [repo](https://github.com/MiroShark/MiroShark)

**x402 role:** Core. An unpaid POST to https://x402.miroshark.xyz/run on 2026-10-11 returned 402, x402 v2, with three options of 1.00 USD USDC each: Base, Solana and Monad. [endpoint](https://x402.miroshark.xyz/run) Docs: the payment is the authentication (no API key); the facilitator sponsors gas; Base and Solana settle through Coinbase CDP, Monad through its own facilitator; failed runs get no automatic refund. Builders who send a code header get 10% of each Base payment through an ownerless 0xSplits contract (x402aff). [docs](https://www.miroshark.xyz/docs), [x402aff](https://www.x402aff.xyz) A blog post dated 2026-07-09 (updated Sep 2026) explains the choice. [post](https://www.miroshark.xyz/blog/what-is-miroshark-and-x402) The x402 origin was registered 2026-06-03 and the first Base payment shows on 2026-07-09. [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com/api/trpc/public.stats.bucketed) A natural fit: anonymous pay-per-run for agents.

**Chains:** Base for the token; x402 on Base, Solana and Monad. [docs](https://www.miroshark.xyz/docs)

**Token:** MIROSHARK; Base 0xd7bc6a05a56655fb2052f742b012d1dfd66e1ba3.
- Created 2026-03-24 16:48:01 UTC (block 43790767) in an ERC-4337 handleOps call on EntryPoint v0.7 sent by EOA 0x57c5115fe9f1cffdf0607a9b9beefa56a9125870, so no factory is visible; a Uniswap v4 pool was created in the same second. [Base tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0xa6788cb998d3c42c3094f59f232cf37cba1f6db52d0e751173dcbcacb646fe45), [GeckoTerminal](https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/networks/base/tokens/0xd7bc6a05a56655fb2052f742b012d1dfd66e1ba3/pools)
- Method: Bankr (the README links a Bankr page and CoinGecko tags it Bankr Ecosystem; the Clanker v4 factory is absent from the tx logs). [README](https://github.com/MiroShark/MiroShark), [CoinGecko](https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/miroshark) Supply 100B. The token's owner() is contract 0x660eaaedebc968f8f3694354fa8ec0b4c5ba8d12, which also owns the $AEON token below (not identified). [Aeon tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x7244f8063d151e5c4136b93ac0ebe72ab0d1b4204a19338f24bcd29e97d6004f)
- Stated utility: none; the README says "support the project" and gives the address, x402 settles in USDC, and the site and docs never describe a token role. [README](https://github.com/MiroShark/MiroShark), [blog](https://www.miroshark.xyz/blog/what-is-miroshark-and-x402)
- Still trading; about 125K USD liquidity. [DexScreener](https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/0xd7bc6a05a56655fb2052f742b012d1dfd66e1ba3)

**Team:** Aaron Elijah Mars: the README says "built by" him, founder of Aeon and MiroShark; his GitHub profile (aaronjmars) uses that name, links aaronjmars.com and declares X handle aaronjmars (high confidence). [README](https://github.com/MiroShark/MiroShark), [profile](https://github.com/aaronjmars) Project X @miroshark_ is linked from the site and README. [site](https://miroshark.xyz) Telegram miroshark_aeon, TikTok miroshark7 and YouTube mirosharkxyz are CoinGecko-listed only. [CoinGecko](https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/miroshark) Doxxed by self-declaration; one visible maintainer.

**Track record and pivots:** 2025-11-26 upstream MiroFish repo created (AGPL-3.0, 78K stars today) [MiroFish](https://github.com/666ghj/MiroFish); 2026-03-04 maintainer's Aeon repo created and 2026-03-10 $AEON created on Base [Aeon repo](https://github.com/aeonfun/aeon), [Aeon tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x7244f8063d151e5c4136b93ac0ebe72ab0d1b4204a19338f24bcd29e97d6004f); 2026-03-20 MiroShark repo created, and on 2026-03-21 its history shows commits renaming MiroFish to MiroShark across the project and swapping Zep Cloud for Neo4j and Ollama [repo commits](https://github.com/MiroShark/MiroShark); 2026-03-24 token created; 2026-03-25 two Aeon "growth agent" repos created, one for MiroShark [maintainer repos](https://github.com/aaronjmars?tab=repositories); 2026-06-03 x402 origin; 2026-07-09 first Base payment; 2026-07-15 repo moved to the MiroShark org; 2026-10-05 x402aff added to the ecosystem list. [changelog](https://www.miroshark.xyz/changelog)

**Trend-hopping score (1-5):** 2. One product since March 2026 and x402 is its payment path. The caution is provenance: a rebrand of a viral repo, a token three days later, and a sibling token two weeks earlier from the same builder. [repo](https://github.com/MiroShark/MiroShark), [Aeon tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x7244f8063d151e5c4136b93ac0ebe72ab0d1b4204a19338f24bcd29e97d6004f)

**Funding:** None found. Revenue seen: about 50 USD of x402 payments on Base. [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com/api/trpc/public.stats.bucketed)

**Product and traction:** GitHub: 1,467 stars, 304 forks, 0 open issues. [repo](https://github.com/MiroShark/MiroShark) The ecosystem page lists 13 projects built on it. [ecosystem](https://www.miroshark.xyz/ecosystem) A Bazaar listing from HivemindOS resells runs at 1.20 USD. [CDP Bazaar discovery API](https://api.cdp.coinbase.com/platform/v2/x402/discovery/resources) x402scan, Base: 51 payments / 50.01 USD from 2026-07-09 to 2026-10-09; 19 payments / 19 USD in the last 30 days. [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com/api/trpc/public.stats.bucketed) Cross-check: the sibling note shows the same 30-day and all-time figures plus a Bazaar count of 48 calls in 30 days, which exceeds the 19 Base payments and was not reconciled (Solana and Monad payments or unpaid probes are possible). [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md)

**Last six months (2026-04-11 to 2026-10-11):** Repo commits per month: Apr 134 (includes Apr 1-10), May 64, Jun 90, Jul 37, Aug 30, Sep 15, Oct 1-10 10. [GitHub](https://github.com/MiroShark/MiroShark) The changelog has 24 entries from 2026-06-21 to 2026-10-05, mostly dependency and security bumps; features include German and French locales, CLI subcommands, an OrcaRouter preset and an open dataset (2026-09-28, 8,201 agent decisions from 16 simulations). [changelog](https://www.miroshark.xyz/changelog), [dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/MiroShark/social-prediction-market-sim) Solana and Monad payment support appears in docs updated 2026-09-29. [docs](https://www.miroshark.xyz/docs)

**Direction:** More builder tooling around x402 (affiliate splits, MCP, dataset). [x402aff](https://www.x402aff.xyz) Opinion: it depends on one maintainer, and revenue is small.

**Competitors:** Upstream MiroFish and forks such as MiroFish-Offline (2.6K stars). [search](https://github.com/666ghj/MiroFish) General knowledge, unsourced: audience-simulation startups such as Aaru and Simile.

**Green and red flags:** Green: open source, steady commits, working multichain x402, honest refund policy, named maintainer. [docs](https://www.miroshark.xyz/docs), [repo](https://github.com/MiroShark/MiroShark) Red: the README does not credit MiroFish while the commit history shows a rename from it, and the repo is not marked as a fork (whether the licence notices carry over is not confirmed) [repo commits](https://github.com/MiroShark/MiroShark); the token has no stated utility; the commit rate fell from 134 to 10; two repos named as growth agents promote the tokens [maintainer repos](https://github.com/aaronjmars?tab=repositories). Not confirmed: code overlap with MiroFish (no diff done); the Bankr factory.

**Verdict:** Credible but small; momentum Slowing. Opinion: the best x402 fit in this group, but small revenue, derived code and a serial token pattern call for care.

#### Profile: SMOL (SMOL)
**One-line:** Multi-chain mascot token from the Treasure ecosystem. smolcoin.lol is a buying guide with a price widget and a promotion of Wiz, an AI character on Treasure's AI Frens platform (the platform page now reads Summon). It has no product of its own. [smolcoin.lol](https://smolcoin.lol)

**x402 role:** A passive payment-token role through Treasure's facilitator, which speaks x402 v1, scheme exact, on Base and Base Sepolia and was live on 2026-10-11. [supported](https://x402.treasure.lol/facilitator/supported) Its page lists SMOL for swaps, and the x402 Foundation's partner data says it supports EIP-3009 tokens including USDC, MAGIC, SMOL, MIO and AI Frens tokens, and lists AI Frens as a way to launch x402-compatible character tokens. [facilitator page](https://x402.treasure.lol), [partner entry](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/x402-foundation/x402/2b74587b29bd7d986c4c562e46444d74606b0e1d/typescript/site/app/ecosystem/partners-data/treasure-facilitator/metadata.json), [AI Frens entry](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/x402-foundation/x402/2b74587b29bd7d986c4c562e46444d74606b0e1d/typescript/site/app/ecosystem/partners-data/treasure-aifrens/metadata.json) On-chain check (eth_call): the Base SMOL token answers the EIP-3009 calls authorizationState and the TransferWithAuthorization typehash, so it can be an x402 asset. [token](https://basescan.org/token/0xa4bbac7ed5bda8ec71a1af5ee84d4c5a737bd875) The SMOL site never mentions x402, and no SMOL-denominated settlement was found because public RPC log queries were blocked.

**Chains:** Base, Ethereum and Abstract; CoinGecko says an earlier contract migrated off Solana, citing a project post dated 2025-07-15 (date from the tweet ID). [CoinGecko](https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/smolcoin-2)

**Token:** SMOL; Base 0xa4bbac7ed5bda8ec71a1af5ee84d4c5a737bd875 (Ethereum 0x53cce6d10e43d1b3d11872ad22ec2acd8d2537b8; Abstract 0xcbfb6dbeb6c06ddcd345781a0adc711a86a77f7c). [CoinGecko](https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/smolcoin-2) Base contract created 2025-10-28 16:36:31 UTC by direct deployment from EOA 0xd1d943c09b9c3355207ce8c85ab1c4558f6cd851 (block 37440022); it is an upgradeable EIP-1967 proxy and its owner() is that same EOA; no launchpad. [Base tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x72b8784d0dedb9f51faaca2d4dbff5c314e7817e3e09a71eae76982016753137) Supply on Base about 309.5M; the main pool is against MAGIC, created 2025-10-29, at about 56K USD with small volume. Utility: none stated, and no real work. [GeckoTerminal](https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/networks/base/tokens/0xa4bbac7ed5bda8ec71a1af5ee84d4c5a737bd875/pools)

#### Observations across these profiles (Aubrai, Ethy AI, Sally A1C, ArAIstotle, ROBA, MiroShark, VPay, SMOL)
- A serial launcher by on-chain pattern: Aaron Elijah Mars's $AEON token was created on Base on 2026-03-10 and $MIROSHARK on 2026-03-24, both through ERC-4337 handleOps on EntryPoint v0.7; both report the same owner() contract, and both READMEs link a Bankr page. The Aeon repo was created 2026-03-04 and MiroShark's 2026-03-20, a day before its rename-from-MiroFish commits; two growth-agent repos created 2026-03-25 describe themselves as promoting the tokens. [Aeon tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x7244f8063d151e5c4136b93ac0ebe72ab0d1b4204a19338f24bcd29e97d6004f), [MiroShark tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0xa6788cb998d3c42c3094f59f232cf37cba1f6db52d0e751173dcbcacb646fe45), [maintainer repos](https://github.com/aaronjmars?tab=repositories)
- Launchpad machinery, not teams: FACY (2025-08-09) and VPAY (2025-07-19) share one factory contract and one sending address (Virtuals' launch machinery); ETHY used an earlier Virtuals contract on 2025-01-17; A1C was created the same day as ETHY, apparently via Creator.Bid per CoinGecko (unconfirmed); AUBRAI used Bio's launchpad, which kept running sales such as PeptAI in May 2026. #### Profile: Trackgood AI (TRAI)
**One-line:** One token over two agents: TrackFi (crypto-Twitter signal trading assistant, alpha) and Traicy/Trackgood.io (ESG "Scan-to-Earn"). [Homepage](https://www.trackgood.ai/)
**x402 role:** Claimed, not shown. Whitepaper V2 and the homepage describe an "x402-compatible" enterprise API for ESG data and trader analytics; the TrackFi app bundle calls a plain API (app-api.trackgood.io) with no x402 strings; the roadmap puts a "developer API for third-party agents" under 2026+; not in the Bazaar pull; the sibling note says "none matched". [Whitepaper](https://trackgood.gitbook.io/docs/llms-full.txt), [Homepage](https://www.trackgood.ai/), [App](https://app.trackfi.ai), [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md)

Supply 1,000,000,000 at 8 decimals; `owner()` is the zero address. Allocation: 70% public, 20% private, 10% team. Stated utility: TrackFi PRO (hold or stake 1M TRAI, or pay monthly), rewards, xTRAI staking and governance (2026+); the app landing shows no TRAI gating. #### Profile: Loky (LOKY)
**One-line:** DappLooker's "agentic data infrastructure" brand: market and on-chain data plus an "Ask Loky" natural-language API for trading agents. [lokyai.com](https://lokyai.com/), [dapplooker.com](https://dapplooker.com/)
**x402 role:** Real and live, on the parent's API. DappLooker's changelog: 2025-10-15 "Added x402 payment support... api_key optional". On 2026-10-11 `GET api.dapplooker.com/v1/trending` returned HTTP 402 (x402 v1, exact) asking 5000 units ($0.005) and `POST /v1/ask-loky` asked 100000 ($0.10), in USDC on Polygon, payee 0x045089293817faa2C84c8F21Fcb407F715F23b55. The pricing page lists "Agent Native Access: Pay-per-call via x402" and the x402 Foundation lists "DappLooker AI" ("native x402 support"). Usage is tiny: x402scan shows the same payee with 2,272 tx / $11.36 on Base from 2026-07-13 to 2026-08-12 (one buyer per day) and nothing on Polygon. The sibling note says "none matched" for lokyai.com, which is a domain-match miss because the API host is api.dapplooker.com. The LOKY token itself takes no x402 payments (not found). [Changelog](https://docs.dapplooker.com/get-started/changelog.md), [x402 doc](https://docs.dapplooker.com/get-started/get-started-with-x402-payments.md), [402 probe](https://api.dapplooker.com/v1/ask-loky), [Foundation metadata](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/x402-foundation/x402/2b74587b29bd7d986c4c562e46444d74606b0e1d/typescript/site/app/ecosystem/partners-data/dapplooker/metadata.json), [x402scan payee](https://www.x402scan.com/recipient/0x045089293817faa2C84c8F21Fcb407F715F23b55), [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md)
**Chains:** Base (token); the live 402 asks for USDC on Polygon; x402scan shows the payee on Base. [402 probe](https://api.dapplooker.com/v1/trending), [x402scan payee](https://www.x402scan.com/recipient/0x045089293817faa2C84c8F21Fcb407F715F23b55)

#### Profile: DOBI (DOBI)
**One-line:** AI-agent token from the Dobprotocol ecosystem; dobi.guru is an "agent-native IoT" platform where AI agents manage device clusters (DAMs) and x402 is meant to settle each action. [dobi.guru](https://dobi.guru/)
**x402 role:** Central to the pitch, wired but unused. The site says "x402 settles every action on-chain" (example 0.04 USDC); llms.txt documents `POST /api/x402/{asset_id}/pay` ("402 Payment Required... or executes if paid"). The project's own `/health` on 2026-10-11 11:41Z reports `x402_stats`: total_sessions 65, paid_sessions 0, expired 65, and `/api/dams/stats` reports 12 DAMs (4 running), 6 devices, total_revenue 0. Not in the Bazaar pull; the sibling note says "none matched" (consistent). The sub-agent did not open a payment session because that would write to their database. [dobi.guru](https://dobi.guru/), [llms.txt](https://dobi.guru/llms.txt), [health](https://dobi.guru/health), [stats](https://dobi.guru/api/dams/stats), [sibling note](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md)

Utility per site: settle x402 sessions in USDC or $DOBI, "continuous $DOBI buyback" from ACP revenue; no paid use evidenced. Thinly traded, DOBI/VIRTUAL liquidity about $33.4K. #### Profile: WachAI (WACH)
**One-line:** Token-verification and "mandates" protocol for AI agents from QuillAI Network (formerly WachXBT), with a pay-per-call TokenSense API over x402. [wach.ai](https://wach.ai/), [QuillAI](https://quillai.network/)
**x402 role:** A real, live seller. `https://x402.wach.ai/verify-token` returns HTTP 402 (x402 v2, scheme exact, USDC on Base, amount 10000 = $0.01, payTo 0x9acfDC5A3D6Bfbb127615751E0F0e44014E32abF) with further payment options; `/openapi.json` is titled "TokenSense x402 API" (GET /verify-token, GET /resolveToken, POST /buyToken, plus parallel "MPP" routes). One Bazaar resource, last updated 2026-09-25T11:26Z. The open-source x402-wach CLI/SDK (MIT, 7 commits) says "Payment is always USDC on Base", so the WACH token is not the payment asset; the site says "WachAI makes x402 trustless through Mandates". #### Profile: AInalyst (AIN)
**One-line:** AI Web3 research and social-recap platform (formerly Statistr), launched via Virtuals Genesis. Its main site is down, and its x402 footprint is a "Click the button" pay-per-click endpoint (AinX402) that peaked in Oct-Dec 2025. [Docs](https://docs.ainalyst.xyz/llms-full.txt)
**x402 role:** AinX402 at ainalyst-api.xyz is the only tie. A third-party catalog snapshot (observed 2026-06-11) lists Bazaar resource `POST http://ainalyst-api.xyz/click`, titled "Click the button", x402 v1, exact, USDC on Base, payee 0x5E50d23EC14Ea4436582029a25313e5048e01D10, price $0.01, Bazaar last updated 2026-02-17, 1 call in 30 days; it is absent from today's Bazaar pull. The AinX402 homepage is a stub titled "Creating the world's knowledge hub" whose script redirects to the DexScreener page of 0x294799c9...6a70, which the sub-agent confirmed on-chain is the VIRTUAL/AIN pool. That suggests the AIN project (or someone pointing at it) runs the endpoint, but AInalyst's own docs never mention x402, the click game or this domain, so the operator link is unconfirmed. Later on 2026-10-11 the domain failed TLS validation from the sub-agent's network and `/click` returned a 403 block page, so the current endpoint state is unknown. #### Observations across these profiles (Maya World, Replicats, MIO, HEIR, TRAI, Loky, StarkBot, DOBI, Arbus, WachAI, AInalyst)
- Shared launch contracts (contract facts only): 0xf66dea7b...3259 created LOKY (2024-12-02), DOBI (2024-12-04) and MAYA (2025-05-07), plus ETHY, BRO and TRUST in this note's sample; 0x42f4f5a3...ea9 created ARBUS (2025-05-18), AIN (2025-05-26) and WACH (2025-06-25), plus FACY, CAP, VPAY and SS, all from one sender, 0x81f7ca6a...1415; the Clanker sender 0x2112b845... launched HEIR (2025-03-23, v3.1) and STARKBOT (2026-01-28, v4). No common ownership is inferred; a launcher service wallet is possible.
- Seven Virtuals-launched tokens (MAYA, RCAT, LOKY, DOBI, ARBUS, WACH, AIN) are 45-byte clones of three implementations and all return `owner()` 0xe220329659d41b2a9f26e83816b424bdacf62567. None of the implementations contains EIP-3009 selectors, so none can be the "exact" scheme's payment asset. HEIR and STARKBOT have ERC-2612 permit only; TRAI has neither; only MIO has EIP-3009 (selector search on 2026-10-11 via the public Base RPC). [Base RPC](https://mainnet.base.org)
- Category label versus evidence: a live 402 response exists for WACH (x402.wach.ai) and LOKY (api.dapplooker.com); DOBI documents an x402 route (not called) and its own stats show 0 paid sessions; MIO has a facilitator tie; STARKBOT had x402 in code until March 2026; AIN has a click-game spike; MAYA, RCAT, ARBUS and HEIR show no x402 activity or code; TRAI only claims it.
- Dated timing around the PING episode: the AinX402 payee's first payments are 2025-10-24, one day after the PING mint; MIO was deployed 2025-10-30; DappLooker added x402 on 2025-10-15, before the spike. [x402scan payee](https://www.x402scan.com/recipient/0x5e50d23ec14ea4436582029a25313e5048e01d10), [Changelog](https://docs.dapplooker.com/get-started/changelog.md)
- Builder-level diversification: the ethereumdegen account created about 25 new repos in Sept 2026 across unrelated tools; HEIR's org spans a DeFAI agent, inheritance contracts, a macOS coding app and Family.Cash; the GitHub account linked from dobi.guru also lists unrelated 2026 experiments (an agent-discovery benchmark, a Solana escrow, a connectome token launcher). [ethereumdegen repos](https://api.github.com/users/ethereumdegen/repos?per_page=100&sort=pushed&direction=desc), [heirlabs repos](https://api.github.com/orgs/heirlabs/repos?per_page=100), [opcastil11 repos](https://api.github.com/users/opcastil11/repos?sort=pushed)
- Roadmap dates passed without visible delivery: Arbus Q4 2025 Data Marketplace; AInalyst Q3-Q4 2025 public API and marketplace; Replicats Q1 2026 Agent Launchpad. [Arbus roadmap](https://docs.arbus.ai/roadmap/product-roadmap/), [AInalyst docs](https://docs.ainalyst.xyz/llms-full.txt), [Replicats docs](https://docs.replicats.ai/llms-full.txt)
- Sibling-note contradictions found: LOKY "none matched" misses the api.dapplooker.com host; the WACH figure is payee-level and the payee is shared; MIO's facilitator footprint sits under MAGIC; STARKBOT's x402 footprint is code, not a seller origin.

#### Profile: Dealer Node (DNODE)
**One-line:** Pay-per-run Docker container execution on Base, paid with x402 USDC. The homepage now pitches "The Execution Layer for Crypto" with DCA tools for token launches, and the API backend did not answer on 2026-10-11. [dealernode.app](https://dealernode.app/)
**x402 role:** Seller by design. The published OpenAPI v2.0 says POST /v2/jobs "Requires payment via the x402 protocol": call without X-PAYMENT to get a 402, sign an EIP-3009 transferWithAuthorization for USDC on Base, retry with X-PAYMENT; the quote example is $2.50 for 3,600 seconds and the facilitator example is x402.org. [OpenAPI](https://docs.dealernode.app/openapi.json) On 2026-10-11 around 12:15 UTC every path tried on the production host backend.dealernode.app returned Railway's fallback "Application not found" (HTTP 404, x-railway-fallback: true), which could be an outage or a retired service. No dealernode domain is in the Bazaar pull. [backend](https://backend.dealernode.app/api/v2/jobs/quote)
**Chains:** Base. [OpenAPI](https://docs.dealernode.app/openapi.json)
**Token:** DNODE; Base; 0xa7f6e8a35d83228cc6c241c8dd0fb687552f75d1. Created 2025-11-24 13:25:01 UTC through 0xc3538ddd84619e761b4c03caf2f785f79889958d (tx sender 0x7ff3b524122590a393543552bb7d1998e5144296), the same proxy as NOD and PRXVT by Virtuals, so a Virtuals launch; GeckoTerminal tags it "Virtuals Protocol". [tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x65a18860ee554adc89f678dca0a5eac1582a77610ad7e2f5b4659fae53b508af), [GeckoTerminal](https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/networks/base/tokens/0xa7f6e8a35d83228cc6c241c8dd0fb687552f75d1/info) The DNODE/VIRTUAL v2 pool has about $20K liquidity and zero 24h volume; 7,986 holders. [GT pools](https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/networks/base/tokens/0xa7f6e8a35d83228cc6c241c8dd0fb687552f75d1/pools?page=1) The site footer labels 0xdc22ece7221293d8e3834ddd02d970a414791d17 as the "Contract Address (Base)", but GeckoTerminal shows that address is the DNODE/VIRTUAL pool, not the token. [dealernode.app](https://dealernode.app/) Stated utility: none on the site or docs; no evidence the token does work in the product.
**Team:** Anonymous; no team section. GitHub dealernode (a User account created 2025-12-10) self-declares twitter_username dealernodeapp and blog dealernode.app, and the site links the same X account (confidence high). [repos](https://api.github.com/users/dealernode/repos), [dealernode.app](https://dealernode.app/), [GeckoTerminal](https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/networks/base/tokens/0xa7f6e8a35d83228cc6c241c8dd0fb687552f75d1/info)
**Trend-hopping score (1-5):** 3. It moved from compute for agents (x402 and ACP) to a trading-flavoured "execution layer", with a real API spec and SDK at the earlier stage. [dealernode.app](https://dealernode.app/)
**Funding:** None found. **Product and traction:** An API specification, a TypeScript SDK and a Discord; no usage data found. [OpenAPI](https://docs.dealernode.app/openapi.json), [repos](https://api.github.com/users/dealernode/repos)
