#### Observations across these profiles (Karum, Otonix, 0xNod, Sniper Search, TRUST, SWARM, ZorbyAI, TMAI, Dealer Node, BluePay, Halo)
- Shared launchers show common platforms, not common teams: 0xc3538d...958d launched DNODE (2025-11-24), PRXVT (2026-01-06) and NOD (2026-02-11); 0x42f4f5...1ea9, sent by one address, launched ARBUS (2025-05-18), AIN (2025-05-26), CAP (2025-05-23), SS (2025-06-08), WACH (2025-06-25), VPAY (2025-07-19) and FACY (2025-08-09); 0xf66dea...3259 launched TRUST (2024-11-29), LOKY, DOBI, BRO, ETHY and MAYA by 2025-05-07. [Base RPC creation data](https://basescan.org)
- Virtuals ACP came before x402 in three projects: Karum built an ACP buyer repo on 2025-07-29, Dealer Node lists ACP as completed beside x402, and Sniper Search lists ACP and x402 together. [acp-integration](https://github.com/Karum-AI/acp-integration), [dealernode.app](https://dealernode.app/), [docs](https://snipersearch.gitbook.io/sniper-search-docs/llms-full.txt)
- Karum described itself three ways in 15 months: agent coordination (July 2025), B2B marketing agents (2026-04-19), Spanish Google/Meta ads (site updated 2026-10-10). [commits](https://api.github.com/repos/Karum-AI/.github/commits?since=2026-04-01T00:00:00Z), [karum.ai](https://www.karum.ai/)
- Otonix went from GitHub account (2026-02-20) to token (2026-02-23) to first repos (2026-02-27) to last push (2026-03-05) to a dead site. [GitHub API](https://api.github.com/users/otonix-ai), [repo](https://api.github.com/repos/otonix-ai/otonix)
- The TRUST builder's GitHub account created EVClaw (2026-02-15), EVPOLY (2026-03-16) and evp-ledger (2026-09-02), and evplus.ai links evp-ledger; TRUST-BOT was last pushed 2025-08-28. [repos](https://api.github.com/users/Degenapetrader/repos), [evplus.ai](https://www.evplus.ai/)
- Dealer Node's site description moved from compute to a trading-flavoured "execution layer" (DCA for token launches), still marked "PREVIEW". [dealernode.app](https://dealernode.app/) Halo and the $JUNO token share the ZHC Institute as incubator; no launch date found for $JUNO. [zhcinstitute.com](https://zhcinstitute.com)
- The sibling note's "none matched" came from matching website domains only and missed live x402 sellers on other hosts: alpha.snipersearch.fun (SS), api.slamai.dev (SWARM, in the Bazaar), x402-api.bluepay.network (BLUE) and backend.dealernode.app (DNODE, defined in its OpenAPI but unreachable); the 402 responses above were plain GET requests and nothing was paid.
- Could not confirm (WebSearch unavailable or sites blocked), per the sub-agent: any funding round, press coverage or audit for all eleven; real names or backgrounds for anonymous teams; TMAI's x402 tie, the meaning of "[OLD]" and whether this is the legacy contract; ZorbyAI's MERV and X activity; Karum's reasons for changing direction; Halo's full x402 endpoint, Bankr launch and @HireHalo handle; X, Farcaster and Telegram activity for every project; Virtuals launch pages (scripted); GitHub was not searched for 0xNod, Sniper Search, The SWARM, Halo or Token Metrics. Public RPC was rate-limited, so no on-chain x402 settlement volumes were measured for any seller.

#### Profile: WalletChan (WCHAN)
**One-line:** Self-custodial EVM browser-extension wallet with AI-agent tooling. WCHAN is a 1:1 wrapper of an older Clanker token, BankrWallet. [site](https://walletchan.com)

[creation tx](https://basescan.org/tx/0x147084435d43943cf7ab88317ad1f274fb35c1e3c00492cd3095f9f1bb2336eb) Supply 100B, wrapping BankrWallet (BNKRW, 0xf48bC234...3b07, Clanker v4, created 2026-01-30). [Blockscout](https://base.blockscout.com/address/0xf48bC234855aB08ab2EC0cfaaEb2A80D065a3b07) The site calls it "the token behind the wallet"; a stake button shows 0%. GeckoTerminal holders 222, top 10 hold 90%; the main pool had about 29 trades in 24h. [GeckoTerminal pools](https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/networks/base/tokens/0xba5ed0000e1ca9136a695f0a848012a16008b032/pools)
**Team:** Pseudonymous. The GitHub org `walletchan` (created 2026-07-29) has no public members. An account named apoorv.eth appears in mockups and testimonials; whether that is the founder is unconfirmed. X @walletchan_ and the GitHub org are linked from the site. [org](https://api.github.com/orgs/walletchan)
**Track record and pivots:** Commit history starts the week of 2026-01-25. The token followed on 2026-02-25. The GitHub repo was created 2026-07-29 and three more repos on 2026-10-06. [repo](https://api.github.com/repos/walletchan/walletchan), [stats](https://api.github.com/repos/walletchan/walletchan/stats/commit_activity)
**Trend-hopping score (1-5):** 2. A real wallet product with consistent focus; agent and x402 tooling are extensions, and the token is a wrapper of a Bankr-era token.
**Funding:** None found. **Product and traction:** Chrome and Firefox extension, docs site and an MCP server. 1,069 commits and 1 star. No user numbers. [repo](https://github.com/walletchan/walletchan)
**Last six months (2026-04-11 to 2026-10-11):** About 519 commits in the weeks 2026-04-12 to 10-04, with pushes on 2026-10-06 and 10-09. [stats](https://api.github.com/repos/walletchan/walletchan/stats/commit_activity)
**Direction:** AI-agent wallet; docs list a Bankr agent chat and Bankr remote signing. [sibling platform note](platform_coinbase_base_protocol.md)
**Green and red flags:** Green: real code, active, open source. Red: anonymous team; thin holders (top 10 hold 90%); the x402 link is new and mostly in MCP tools.

#### 3E. Category members without a Base contract (excluded)
The other 73 members of CoinGecko's x402 Ecosystem category (for example UB/Unibase, CCD/Concordium, BKN/Brickken, ADX/heyAura, PAYAI, CODEC, AMIKO, ZAUTH, NODE, CORAL, XONA, DEXTER, ELIZA, and new 2026 entries on Arc and Robinhood Chain such as CRA, FUCI, MESH, DICE) have no Base contract on CoinGecko and are out of scope; the full list with chains is in the sibling note's section 1.3b. [ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md](ecosystem_map_and_metrics.md)

### Inferences
- The x402 tag was mostly applied to existing AI-agent tokens: 30 of the 47 triaged tokens predate PING's deployment and 15 predate x402 itself. Tokens built for x402 after PING (x420, REGENT, SLINKY, VAPI, PRXVT, ELSA's Base token, LOOP and a few more) are a minority, and the x402-named meme cluster is far larger by count (127 pools) than by any measure of use. Token Metrics AI [OLD] shows how tags persist: the x402 label sits on a legacy Base listing while the company's homepage promotes a Solana mint.
- Having a real product is common (30 of 47) but having x402 matter to it is rare. Where x402 is central (PRXVT, Slinky Layer, vAPI, WachAI, Sniper Search, MiroShark, the SWARM's SLAMai, AlphaKEK), measured revenue is still tiny or unmeasured; where revenue is largest (AInalyst's AinX402), the pattern looks scripted and the site is down.
- Name-squatting follows attention: pool creation spikes in the two weeks after PING (34 in 2025-10, 23 in 2025-11) and again in early 2026-02 when "claw" names were popular (25 x402claw tokens in 12 days); it never reached a product. In July 2026 a new B20 launcher produced two more x402/HTTP-named tokens within 15 days of its verification.
- Several verdicts depend on pages that were unreadable or undated, so "Stalled" and "Slowing" are best read as "no dated evidence of activity" rather than proof of inactivity.

### Gaps
- Team, product and activity verdicts for the 3A rows come from the profiles in 3F and section 2; where a profile says "unconfirmed" that label carries over. Funding was mostly not found, and X, Telegram and Discord posts could not be read, so 2026 activity on those channels is unknown for every project.
- GeckoTerminal's category endpoint requires a paid key and its web page only shows the top 20 pools; CoinMarketCap's x402 list adds names (Quack AI, Sora Oracle, Litebeam, Agentic Open Economy) whose Base presence I did not verify.
- DexScreener and GeckoTerminal searches cannot enumerate all x402-named tokens; the 130 found are a lower bound, and 70 of the 96 Clanker rows in 3D were attributed to Clanker by the "b07" address suffix without opening the creation transaction.
- Some sites failed TLS validation or returned block pages from the research machines later in the session (for example mayaworld.co, trackgood.ai, starkbot.ai, hivra.cloud, 402rush.fun); they were not bypassed, and facts about them come from earlier fetches or other sources.

## 4. What did the token wave do to x402 usage numbers? Share of mints at the peak and now

### Takeaway
Token mints were a large share of x402 transactions for about one week and a rounding error before and after: on x402scan's Base data, the identified $1 mint payees were 25% of the week of 2025-10-20 (50% if probable mints are added; PING alone was 97% of 2025-10-22), then 1% in the week after, near zero from December 2025, and about 0% in the last 30 days. The much bigger surge that followed (peak month 2025-11: 64.3M Base transactions; peak day 2025-11-15: 3.79M) was not mints; ten recipients account for 85% of all-time Base transactions, and a Visa and Artemis analysis later put adjusted cumulative volume far below the raw totals.

### Cited Findings
- Method (mine, reproducible): I pulled x402scan's public tRPC endpoints (`public.stats.bucketed` for Base daily buckets, overall and per recipient; `public.sellers.all.list` sorted by unique buyers, all-time, 30-day and 7-day) on 2026-10-11, then checked on the Base RPC whether each high-buyer recipient with a roughly $1 average payment is an ERC-20 token contract. "Verified mints" = 7 recipients that are token contracts paid by x402: PING, BIP "BuildInPublic", x402 (0x4c15...2633), MOOM, 叉肆零贰, x420DOG, MAI "Marcus". Six of the seven use PING's mint template or a factory variant of it; 0x4c15 is a plain ERC-20 that only collected payments (section 2). "Probable mints" = 14 further recipients with exactly-$1 average payments, thousands of payers and a lifespan of a few days (externally owned accounts or non-ERC-20 contracts, so their purpose is unconfirmed). Source for all numbers in this section unless another link is shown: [x402scan public API, bucketed stats](https://www.x402scan.com/api/trpc/public.stats.bucketed) and [sellers list](https://www.x402scan.com/api/trpc/public.sellers.all.list). Scope: Base chain, all facilitators; x402scan's own counts, not an official total.
- Daily peak of mints: on 2025-10-22 PING received 124,095 of 127,661 Base x402 transactions (97.2%). On 2025-10-24 verified mints were 27% of the day, and verified plus probable mints were 60% of 2025-10-25. After 2025-10-29 the verified share was under 1% a day.
- Weekly shares (Monday-start weeks, x402scan Base): week of 2025-10-20: 1,359,622 transactions, of which verified mints 344,523 (25.3%) and probable mints 340,630 (25.1%); week of 2025-10-27: 6,023,744 transactions, verified 66,353 (1.1%), probable 168,400 (2.8%); week of 2025-11-03: 8,974,630 total, verified about 0%, probable 1.3%; week of 2025-11-10: 18,839,864 total, probable 1.4% (one $1 payee took about 181,861 payments on 2025-11-10/11); weeks from 2025-11-17 onward: about 0%.
- Whole-period shares: verified mints were 411,515 of 168,792,862 all-time Base transactions (0.24%) and $553k of $46.9M (1.2%); including probable mints, 1,304,574 transactions (0.8%) and $1.45M (3.1%). From 2026-01-01 to 2026-10-10 verified mints total 3 transactions and probable mints 7,285 (one $1 payee on 2026-02-19), out of 57,449,603 (0.01%). In the last 30 days (about 2.17M Base transactions, $702k, 35,307 payers) only two of the top 200 recipients by payer count had a roughly $1 average (551 and 477 payments, both externally owned accounts); neither is a token contract. [x402scan overall, 30 days](https://www.x402scan.com/api/trpc/public.stats.overall)
- The wave in context (x402scan Base, monthly transactions, my sums): 2025-08: 13,815; 2025-09: 39,525; 2025-10: 4,065,215; 2025-11: 64,261,645 (peak; $27.9M raw); 2025-12: 42,952,297; 2026-01: 12,129,966; 2026-02: 1,419,820; 2026-03: 1,820,036; 2026-04: 2,298,564; 2026-05: 3,041,287; 2026-06: 12,801,179; 2026-07: 11,928,212; 2026-08: 9,071,303; 2026-09: 2,225,349. Peak day 2025-11-15 with 3,785,406 transactions; peak value day 2025-11-03 at $3.0M. Cumulative through 2026-03-31 is about 126.7M by my sums. [x402scan](https://www.x402scan.com/api/trpc/public.stats.bucketed)
- Concentration: the top recipient holds 18.7% of all-time Base transactions, the top 3 hold 52.6%, the top 10 hold 85.1%, the top 100 hold 96.8%; the largest (31.5M transactions) has only 340 unique payers. [x402scan sellers list](https://www.x402scan.com/api/trpc/public.sellers.all.list)
- Independent second dataset (scope not stated on the page): a Dune dashboard by @programmer shows weekly "transfers" of 5,257 (week of 2025-10-06), 26,406 (10-13), 932,440 (10-20, 61,290 buyers but only 372 sellers), 4,600,108 (10-27, 79,271 buyers, the buyer peak), 13,703,302 (11-10, the transfer peak) and 13,659,056 (11-17), then about 5-7M a week in December 2025, 29,256 (2026-01-19), 411k-571k (April 2026), 1.67M (2026-06-08) and 221,104 (2026-08-10). [Dune @programmer/x402](https://dune.com/programmer/x402)
- Early press tallies: x402scan seven-day figures on 2025-10-24 were 163,600 transactions (+701.7%), about $140,200 volume (+8,218.5%) and about 31,000 buyers; the 30-day totals were more than 213,000 transactions and $191,000. [Coinfomania via cryptonews](https://cryptonews.net/news/altcoins/31852534/) By 2025-10-26 cumulative x402scan transactions were 1.446M, with the surge week 63.8% of them, buyers 74,000 and sellers 1,405. [PANews](https://panews.io/articles/7c3be5b3-b0de-4405-adbd-af9a16d4ae09) Another outlet reported "nearly 500,000 transactions between October 14 and 20, a 10,780% increase" and a record 156,492 weekly transactions; the two figures cover different windows and are not reconciled in the article. [Jinse via Bitget, 2025-11-11](https://www.bitget.com/news/detail/12560605057035)
- Chainalysis (2026-06-03, Base): more than 100 million cumulative x402 transactions through Q1 2026; PING had over 150,000 transactions in its first month; the Q4 2025 surge was "over 10,000% in a single week", "driven largely by meme coin activity, especially PING"; a PING-driven rush lifted weekly wallet retention to 87% for one week, then it fell to 5%; transactions of $1 or more rose from 49% to 95% of value moved between early 2025 and early 2026. [Chainalysis](https://www.chainalysis.com/blog/x402-agentic-payments-adoption/) Secondary coverage repeats these figures without a PING share. [crypto.news, 2026-06-04](https://crypto.news/coinbases-x402-has-processed-over-100-million-transactions-on-base/); [TFTC, 2026-06-23](https://www.tftc.io/agentic-payments-x402-above-one-dollar-chainalysis/)
- Adjusted view: a Visa and Artemis analysis, reported by The Defiant, puts adjusted cumulative x402 volume at about $19M, excluding identified wash and test activity (figures as of 2026-04-21), and finds roughly 4,000 wallets (the top 1% of buyers) drive about 90% of adjusted spending, with Base the bulk of it. [The Defiant](https://thedefiant.io/news/infrastructure/visa-s-sheffield-pegs-adjusted-x402-volume-at-19m) (the page body did not load; the headline and summary were readable; "about 134M transactions" and "422,000 wallets" come from a search-result summary only and are unconfirmed).
- All-chain "now" figures: x402.org's live widget (page last updated 2026-08-25) shows, for the last 30 days, 75.41M transactions, $24.24M volume, 94.06K buyers and 22K sellers. [x402.org](https://www.x402.org/) Crypto Briefing (2026-09-15) says Solana carried about 70% of monthly x402 volume in mid-August 2026 and quotes "$24.24 million" as Solana's 30-day volume, which is the same figure x402.org shows for all chains, so that attribution looks wrong or coincidental (unconfirmed which is right). [Crypto Briefing](https://cryptobriefing.com/solana-x402-market-share-dominance/) The Dune "x402 Analytics" dashboard by @hashed_official shows daily transactions by project, with PayAI peaking at 1,720,827 on 2026-09-01 and about 5,200 on 2026-10-10, and Polygon at about 165k a day. [Dune @hashed_official](https://dune.com/hashed_official/x402-analytics)
- Independent sample of Base (2026-08-23 to 2026-09-25): the open dataset "x402-census" finds the recurring economy is 15 payees taking 63.9% of sampled payments but 1.0% of sampled dollars, and says most USDC dollar volume in the same transfer function is ordinary large transfers, not x402. [GitHub savecharlie/x402-census](https://github.com/savecharlie/x402-census)
- Supply side today: the Coinbase CDP Bazaar discovery listing returns 32,170 x402 resources (2026-10-11). Of those, 31,666 list Base; 300 (0.93%) mention "mint" (mostly Solana token-mint-address scanners); only 7 (0.02%) describe launching or deploying a token; one domain (market.datapackvibe.com) holds 8,447 listings (26%). [CDP Bazaar discovery API](https://api.cdp.coinbase.com/platform/v2/x402/discovery/resources?limit=500&offset=0) (my regex counts of the listing descriptions.)
- CoinGecko's category effect: the x402 Ecosystem category started on 2025-10-25 with 10 tokens worth $178.3M and grew to 44 tokens worth $832M by 2025-10-28. [Bitcoin.com via search snippet; page not opened](https://news.bitcoin.com/tl/ano-ang-x402-tokens-ang-sektor-ng-ai-micropayments-ay-tumalon-mula-178m-hanggang-832m-sa-loob-ng-3-araw/) On 2026-10-11 it lists 135 tokens worth $10.87B in total, but $9.85B of that is Chainlink and $0.24B EigenCloud; the other 133 are worth $0.79B combined, 109 of the 135 are under $1M, and 68 are under $100k. [CoinGecko API](https://api.coingecko.com/api/v3/coins/markets?vs_currency=usd&category=x402-ecosystem&per_page=250&page=1)

### Inferences
- The "PING moment" was a short, loud event: PING's mint is about 0.08% of all-time Base x402 transactions but almost all of one day. Its real effect was attention and a copycat wave, not volume.
- The big numbers in November 2025 came from a few payees receiving millions of small payments, not from token mints; mint share was already about 1% while totals were still climbing. Headline x402 transaction counts therefore overstate organic use, consistent with the Visa and Artemis adjusted figures and with the x402-census author's view.
- Today the mint share is effectively zero on x402scan's Base data, and token-launch services are a vanishing fraction of what sellers list in the Bazaar. The token activity that remains happens mostly outside x402 (Clanker and Bankr launches under x402-themed names, section 3).
- The all-chain x402.org total (75.41M transactions in 30 days as of 2026-08-25) and the Base-only x402scan total for the last 30 days (about 2.17M) can both be right; the gap suggests Solana or other chains dominate raw counts in 2026, but I did not verify chain splits.
- Most of the USDC behind the mint-share spike did not become API revenue or lasting liquidity: about 75% of the roughly $603k that left the seven mint contracts was withdrawn by their admin or owner accounts, and about 25% went into two Uniswap pools (section 2).

### Gaps
- No source gives an official "mint versus API payment" split; the shares above are my classification of x402scan recipients and may miss mints sent to externally owned accounts or on other chains. Mints on Solana, X Layer and BNB Chain are not included.
- The identity and purpose of the ten largest recipients (85% of all-time Base transactions) is outside this note (other researchers cover the infrastructure layer).
- Visa and Artemis methodology and the 134M and 422,000 figures could not be opened; the Dune dashboards do not state their scope.
- Whether the Chainalysis dataset counts the same transactions as x402scan is unknown (Chainalysis: over 100M through Q1 2026; x402scan Base cumulative through 2026-03-31 is about 126.7M by my sums, which is consistent but not proof of the same scope).

## 5. Which teams show a dated pattern of narrative hopping with thin delivery?

### Takeaway
The clearest pattern is at the level of the market: CoinGecko's x402 category is mostly older AI-agent tokens relabelled (30 of 47 triaged Base tokens predate PING), and the x402-named token supply on Base spiked twice and then died. Among named teams, the scores in the table below (63 profiles) put 11 at 5 (the mint family and name-only tokens), 13 at 4, 23 at 3 and 16 at 2. The documented cases with several narratives in a short span and thin delivery are small, founder-led or newly formed: x420's repo owner (a new theme every few months since 2024, then a launchpad one day after PING that took payments for three weeks), APINow.fun's founder (a new theme every two to four months since 2024, now a marketplace with $228 of lifetime x402 revenue), Capminal (five narratives in 17 months, $11 of measured x402 revenue), vAPI Network (a July 2026 entity that stacked x402, ERC-8004, MCP, agent wallets and a token before its products), Otonix (token three days after the GitHub account, silence after 2026-03-05), AInalyst (first x402 payments one day after PING, site down), $TRUST ME BROs (builder moved to OpenClaw, Polymarket and a Robinhood Chain token while its own V4 stayed "coming soon") and Halo (agents, x402, Bankr and bounties with no results). Larger names (OpenServ, HeyElsa, AlphaKEK, Regents Labs, Covalent, Heurist's vending machine) pivot with the market but ship real code each time, so they score around 3 or below. Many name-squat tokens come from shared launch wallets and cannot be pinned on any team.

### Cited Findings
- Dated name-squat waves of x402-named tokens on Base: 1 in 2025-05, 1 in 2025-08, 34 in 2025-10, 23 in 2025-11, 9 in 2026-01 and 42 in 2026-02 (including 25 "x402claw"-style tokens in 12 days), then a trickle of 1 to 5 a month; 96 of 127 tabulated are Clanker deployments. [GeckoTerminal search](https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/search/pools?query=x402&network=base&page=1); see section 3D.
- Shared launch wallets, not teams: 0xd9acd656... made 307 Clanker deployments from 2025-03 to 2026-07 ("x402" on 2025-05-07, "x420" on 2025-10-23, "402"/"X402" tokens on 2025-10-24, "Lobster Ax402" on 2026-02-01, "B20" tokens on 2026-07-08); 0x2112b845... made 113 (an "x402" on 2025-08-03, "CDPx402" on 2025-10-30, "X402SCAN" on 2026-01-30, many "Claw/Molt" tokens in 2026-01 and 2026-02, StarkBot on 2026-01-28); 0x0949ed2c... made 4,738. [Clanker API](https://www.clanker.world/api/tokens/fetch-deployed-by-address?address=0xd9acd656a5f1b519c9e76a2a6092265a74186e58)
- Relabelled tokens: 15 of the 47 triaged Base tokens were created before x402's launch (2025-05-06), for example TRUST (2024-11-29), LOKY (2024-12-02), DOBI (2024-12-04), BRO/Neurobro (2024-12-08), TRAI (2024-12-16), HINT (2024-12-17), FLOCK (2024-12-20), ETHY and A1C (2025-01-17), RCAT (2025-01-21), CDX (2025-02-11), SWARM (2025-03-06), HEIR (2025-03-23), TMAI (2024-11-19) and AIKEK (2024-06-17). [Base RPC creation data, 2026-10-11](https://basescan.org)
- x420's repo owner (public GitHub, self-declared profile): Stacks relayer (2024-01), sOrdinals wrapper (2024-03), Bitte agents (2024-12 to 2025-04), Supra game (2025-06), hackathon app and an MCPay fork (2025-08), x420 (token 2025-10-23, repo 2025-10-25), AI shell CLI (2026-02), OpenClaw forks (2026-04); last x402 payment to the x420 payee 2025-11-12, last x420 commit 2026-03-25. [GitHub](https://github.com/markeljan?tab=repositories)
- APINow.fun's founder (self-declared GitHub and Farcaster, official site): `bitcloutoffers` (2021-04), `genai` (2024-05), `aidd` and `repochat.ai` (2024-10), `apinow-sdk` (2025-01), TRANSLATE token (2025-05-29), `x402-brandkit` (2025-10-22), `gmclaw` (2026-02-05), VINI token (2026-02-13), $APINOW (2026-02-25), `hammock-ai`, `fastforms` and `odoai` (2026-03), and `balance` on MPP rather than x402 (2026-08-24). Lifetime x402 revenue of the platform wallet is $228 and npm downloads are 215 in 30 days. [GitHub](https://github.com/1dolinski)

Real code each time, which is why the score is 3. [regents.sh](https://regents.sh/about)

[Heurist repo](https://github.com/heurist-network/x402-vending-machine), [Covalent blog](https://www.covalenthq.com/blog/speedrun-is-going-live-today-with-clanker-and-x402-integration/)
- Operators of the seven mint contracts: each deployer or owner withdrew the contract's USDC within 3 to 27 days (section 2); that is dated and on-chain, but it describes custody rather than narrative-hopping, and no person is identified.
- Real teams added "claw" repos in the same weeks as the claw token spike: HeyElsa created elsa-openclaw on 2026-02-02 and openserv-labs created openclaw-voice-avatar on 2026-02-16 (the point is only the timing). [GitHub HeyElsa](https://github.com/HeyElsa); [GitHub openserv-labs](https://github.com/openserv-labs)

### Inferences
- "Thin delivery" is easiest to document for the name-squat and mint tokens (no site, no repo, no volume, funds withdrawn) and for founder-led micro-projects with small repos and tiny measured revenue. The larger names (OpenServ, HeyElsa, Regents, Covalent, AlphaKEK) show pivots but real shipping, so they sit at 3 or below.
- Because the biggest on-chain serial deployers are shared launch wallets, only documented public records (repos, sites, dated announcements) can support a "team hops narratives" claim; wallet counts alone cannot.
- The sharpest timing signals are within days of PING (x420 token, Heurist's vending machine repo, MIO, the AinX402 payments, the mint family) and in the claw wave of 2026-02. Timing alone is not evidence of intent; the scores weigh it against what each team actually shipped.

### Gaps
- Funding is mostly not found, so I cannot say whether any hopping was financed by token sales or investors.
- X, Telegram and Discord activity could not be read, so a team's 2026 announcements may be missing from its timeline.
- No wallet or pseudonymous person has been linked to a real-world identity, so serial launchers behind the anonymous tokens remain unidentified by design.
- Several scores are low confidence because the evidence was a single page or an undated roadmap; the profiles say which.
